Result: 获得了鲁棒的模型：对中小规模问题能快速给出最优解，对大规模（>200）问题能快速给出次优解。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习方法能够有效应对带容量和时间窗约束的取送货路径优化问题，在求解速度方面具有显著优势，为实际应用中的实时路径规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The task of constructing vehicles optimal routes for pickup and delivery of goods is one of most promising tasks in the context of global urban population growth. Although this kind of problems with small size can be solved by various classical approaches, a fast (or realtime) route optimizer under the constraints of the real world (such as capacity and time windows constraints) for medium-large size problems still remains a highly challenging task. In this work we, for the first time, successfully applied a deep Reinforcing Learning approach (modified JAMPR model) to solve Pickup and Delivery problem with Capacity and Time Window constraints (CPDPTW). We obtained a robust model that gives a fast optimal solution for problems of small and medium size, and gives fast suboptimal solution for problems of larger (> 200) size.

</details>


### [47] [Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177)
*Xuanmian He,Can Li,Wanjing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构引导的时空注意力图神经网络（SGSAN），通过显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG）作为结构先验，并引入基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制将动态时空注意力锚定到该结构上，同时采用解耦的两阶段优化框架解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的冲突，在多个真实数据集上实现了最先进的预测精度与内建可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时空模型虽在交通流量预测中表现优异，但其内在缺乏透明度，现有事后诊断方法受虚假相关性困扰，难以揭示模型真实的决策机制，导致可解释性欠佳且运营可信度不足，制约了其在安全关键型城市系统中的部署。

Method: SGSAN包含三大核心组件：(1) 显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG）以识别交通状态的宏观看传播路径（区别于传统无约束自适应图）；(2) 基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制，将模型的动态时空注意力锚定到结构先验上，使注意力推理与宏观看依赖对齐并防止过度依赖瞬时局部噪声；(3) 解耦的两阶段优化框架，解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的根本冲突。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SGSAN在预测精度上达到最先进的水平，同时提供内建可解释性，并且这种可解释性有机地与交通网络的物理逻辑相吻合。

Conclusion: SGSAN通过结构引导的注意力机制与解耦优化框架，在保持顶级预测性能的同时实现了内建可解释性，其解释结果与交通网络的物理传播逻辑天然一致，为安全关键型城市系统中深度时空模型的可信部署提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Deep spatiotemporal models integrating graph convolutions and attention mechanisms have demonstrated excellent performance in network-level traffic flow prediction, owing to their exceptional ability to capture complex spatiotemporal dependencies. Despite their predictive success, deployment of such models in safety-critical urban systems remains constrained by their inherent lack of transparency. Existing post-hoc diagnostic methods often struggle with spurious correlations and fail to unveil the intrinsic decision-making mechanisms governing traffic dynamics, resulting in suboptimal interpretability and limited operational trustworthiness. To address these challenges, this paper proposes the Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network (SGSAN). Departing from traditional architectures that rely on unconstrained adaptive graphs, SGSAN explicitly learns a static Directed Dependency Graph (DDG) to identify the invariant macroscopic propagation paths of traffic states. We further introduce an InfoNCE-based soft-coupling mechanism that anchors the model's dynamic spatiotemporal attention to this structural prior, offering a mechanistic account of the model's decision-making process while ensuring robust forecasting by aligning attention-based reasoning with identified macroscopic dependencies and preventing over-reliance on ephemeral local noise. Furthermore, a decoupled two-stage optimization framework is developed to resolve the fundamental conflict between structural discovery and predictive error minimization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that SGSAN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while providing built-in interpretability that organically aligns with the physical logic of traffic networks.

</details>


### [48] [KV Cache Compression Through the Lens of Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191)
*Hannah Laus,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Hao Wang,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力感知变换编码（AATC）方法用于KV缓存量化压缩。通过证明白噪声量化模型下注意力感知失真可分解为键和值的加性贡献，并利用变换编码和逆向注水算法在校准集上分配比特以最小化注意力感知失真。在多种基座模型和基准测试上，该方法在约5.8倍压缩下实现近无损精度，优于现有均匀量化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存存储过往token信息，是长上下文推理中的主要内存瓶颈。现有量化方法用低精度数据类型统一表示KV缓存，仅设计最小化缓存本身重建误差的量化方案，未考虑误差如何通过注意力机制传播，导致量化方案在注意力层面对精度的影响未被充分优化。

Method: 1) 理论上证明在白噪声量化模型下，注意力感知失真可分解为键和值的加性贡献，且该贡献在token和通道维度上可因子化。2) 基于信号处理和率失真理论中的变换编码与逆向注水思想，提出AATC方法，在标定集上分配比特以最小化注意力感知失真。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上，覆盖LongBench、RULER、GSM8K、MMLU-Pro和MATH-500基准，该方法在约5.8倍压缩下实现近无损精度，而各基线方法在至少部分设点场景中出现性能退化。

Conclusion: AATC通过将注意力机制的特性纳入量化目标的设计，实现了比现有均匀量化方法更高的压缩率与精度保持，证明了将误差传播特性引入KV缓存量化设计的理论价值和实际有效性。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache stores information from past tokens and is a major memory bottleneck in long-context inference. Existing quantization methods address this bottleneck by representing the KV cache uniformly with lower-precision data types and designing quantization schemes to minimize reconstruction error in the cache itself, without accounting for how that error propagates through attention mechanisms. We prove that, under a white-noise quantization model, the expected attention-aware distortion decomposes into additive key and value contributions that factor across tokens and channels. Building on transform coding and reverse water-filling, which are classical tools from signal processing and rate-distortion theory, we introduce Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC), which allocates bits over a calibration set to minimize attention-aware distortion. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, evaluated across LongBench, RULER, GSM8K, MMLU-Pro, and MATH-500, our method achieves near-lossless accuracy at approximately $5.8\times$ compression, whereas each baseline degrades in at least some settings.

</details>


### [49] [MINT: A Universal Zero-Shot Predictor for Transaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14198)
*Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Julia Rozanova,David Sutton,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT（Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions）框架，通过将预训练的交易序列编码器与仅解码器LLM连接，结合轻量级嵌入注入、交易-语言对齐和指令微调，实现交易数据上的零样本预测问答，在分布内和分布外任务中均达到SOTA性能，同时显著降低输入token、延迟和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行使用交易序列数据进行欺诈预防、信用风险评估和个性化推荐等任务。虽然已有的支付基础模型能编码交易序列为上下文嵌入，但这些模型不支持灵活的零样本推理。现有的基于LLM的方法要么未能充分利用交易数据中的预测信号，要么依赖昂贵的文本序列化或难以扩展的任务特定架构。因此需要一种既能充分利用交易嵌入信息又支持零样本推理的框架。

Method: MINT框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 轻量级嵌入注入，将预训练的交易序列编码器生成的紧凑嵌入注入到仅解码器LLM中；(2) 交易-语言对齐机制，将交易嵌入表示与自然语言语义对齐；(3) 指令微调，使模型能够处理多种下游预测任务的问答。整体框架将交易序列编码器与LLM以多模态方式结合，避免文本序列化带来的高成本。

Result: MINT在分布内和分布外问题的预测问答任务中均达到最优性能，相比文本序列化基线大幅减少了输入token数量、降低了延迟和内存消耗。通过综合的表示分析、对齐策略、训练数据和历史长度分析，验证了紧凑交易嵌入相比文本序列化是更优的交易表示方式。

Conclusion: 紧凑的交易嵌入比文本序列化更适合用于多模态推理和零样本预测任务。MINT框架证明了将交易序列编码器与LLM通过轻量级对齐方式结合的有效性，为金融领域基础模型的零样本预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Banks analyse sequential financial transaction data to perform many tasks, including fraud prevention, credit risk assessment and offer personalization. To improve the predictive accuracy of these tasks, Payments Foundation Models encode transaction sequence data as rich contextual embeddings, which can then be provided to task-specific models as features. However, these Foundation Models are not designed for flexible zero-shot reasoning across novel downstream prediction tasks, limiting their adaptability and utility. Existing LLM-based approaches to zero-shot prediction often fail to fully exploit the predictive signal within transaction data, while relying on costly text serialization or task-specific architectures that scale poorly. To address these limitations, we present the Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions (MINT), a framework that connects a pretrained transaction sequence encoder to a decoder-only LLM through lightweight embedding injection, transaction-language alignment, and instruction tuning. We find that MINT achieves state-of-the-art predictive question-answering performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution questions, while substantially reducing input tokens, latency, and memory consumption compared to text-serialization baselines. Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [50] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性指标在进化过程中选择性保护已构建的重要特征，防止有效特征因遗传算子干扰而丢失，从而提升符号回归及更广泛搜索任务的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化特征构建方法在符号回归中表现良好，但缺乏显式机制来保留进化过程中发现的重要构建特征。遗传算子可能破坏有效的特征，导致有价值的遗传物质丢失，从而降低解的质量。

Method: 提出一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量选择性保护进化过程中构建的特征。该机制对更重要特征提供更强的保护，同时允许较不重要的特征被修改，并从中引入重要特征中有用的构建块。在多个基学习器和多种特征重要性计算方法下进行评估。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上，所提机制一致性地提升了基线方法的解质量；在两个信用分类数据集上，该方法也有效地扩展了超越符号回归的搜索效能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过特征重要性引导的自适应保护机制，有效防止了有效特征在进化过程中的丢失，在回归和分类任务上均表现出鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [51] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTFM，一种将公平性约束直接嵌入表格基础模型（TFM）训练的方法，通过合成公平性任务和基于梯度反转层的公平性感知架构，使模型在单次前向传播中即可实现公平预测，在132个公平性任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）凭借上下文学习能力在表格预测任务中表现出色并被广泛应用于高风险决策场景，但其公平性属性尚未得到充分研究。现有公平性技术无法直接应用于TFM的上下文学习范式，且训练数据中敏感属性访问受限，这两大挑战促使作者探索将公平性约束直接融入TFM训练的方法。

Method: FairTFM采用两方面的策略：(1) 基于合成公平性任务的可扩展训练策略，解决训练数据中敏感属性访问受限的问题；(2) 使用梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）的公平性感知架构，鼓励模型学习对敏感属性不变的表示，从而实现单次前向传播中的公平预测。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，FairTFM在保持竞争性准确率的同时，实现了公平性的一致提升。

Conclusion: FairTFM成功地将公平性约束融入TFM训练过程，验证了在不牺牲模型性能的前提下提升表格基础模型公平性的可行性，为高风险场景下TFM的负责任部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连接子空间聚类问题（Connected Subspace Clustering），在给定连接图约束下将高维数据点划分为k个连通簇，使各簇与m'维仿射子空间的总平方距离最小。论文证明即使在m'=0和带洞网格图情况下也是NP难的，并提出基于Lloyd风格的迭代合并启发式算法，在地球重力测量海平面时间序列数据中优于现有方法，能提取与气候指数对齐的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大地测量学应用启发——需要对海平面进行主成分分析时识别的连续海面区域。传统聚类方法可能产生物理上不连通的分片，而带约束的聚类需要兼顾簇内相似性与空间连通性。现有无约束方法最多留下1966个不连通碎片，无法满足实际物理连续性需求。

Method: 1) 形式化定义Connected Subspace Clustering问题：给定高维点和连通图，划分k个连通簇，最小化到簇最优m'维仿射子空间的平方距离；2) 理论分析证明该问题在m'=0和带洞网格图上NP难且难以在Ω(n^{1/2-ε})内近似；3) 提出Lloyd风格启发式算法，交替进行子空间拟合和迭代合并步骤以强制连通性。

Result: 算法通过构造保证返回恰好k个连通区域，而无约束方法留下多达1966个不连通碎片且代价更高。在160个全局海平面时间序列配置研究中，基于合并的修复策略在73.75%的情况下是最强的四种策略之一，并持续优于(连通的)Ward法等竞争者。所得到的区域能隔离与厄尔尼诺-南方涛动、印度洋偶极子等气候指数对齐的信号。

Conclusion: 提出的连接子空间聚类方法在保留空间连通性的同时获得了更好的聚类质量，在大地测量学中成功提取与已知气候模式对齐的信号。该方法虽为大地测量开发，但适用于其他空间嵌入的多变量时间序列，如气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [53] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSchema，一个无需训练的通用实时schema grounding框架，使语言模型代理能够直接从当前SPARQL端点获取schema证据来查询知识图谱，在多个生物医学KGQA基准和化学知识图谱迁移任务上优于TogoMCP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生命科学知识图谱SPARQL查询依赖TogoMCP等工具提供的Metadata Interoperability Exchange（MIX）文件来辅助语言模型代理。但这些文件的创建和维护仍需要人工参与，成本高且难以扩展到未覆盖的资源。作者希望探索"实时schema grounding"范式，让代理直接从当前端点获取schema证据，避免预先构建和维护curated schema文件的负担。

Method: AutoSchema是一个无需训练的通用框架，核心流程包括：(1) 检查实时schema以获取当前端点的元数据；(2) 将问题中的实体名称映射到图谱标识符；(3) 探索关系路径寻找实体间的语义连接；(4) 在迭代查询构造过程中发现不同资源之间的潜在关联。该方法不依赖预训练或预编译的schema文件，而是动态地从目标端点提取所需的schema信息。

Result: 在Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA和Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA任务中，AutoSchema的平均事实性准确率优于TogoMCP；在Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA（基于BioASQ Task B）评估中取得一致的性能提升。同时，AutoSchema降低了迭代预算耗尽率，在核心评估中的平均工具调用次数更少。在化学知识图谱迁移研究中，AutoSchema展示了能够支持不规则和此前未见过的RDF图谱的能力，无需先创建curated schema文件。

Conclusion: 实时schema grounding是一种有效且可扩展的替代方案，能够替代预先维护的MIX文件方法。AutoSchema证明了无需训练、直接利用实时端点schema信息即可支持跨资源的知识图谱查询，并在新技术领域（如化学KG）展现了良好的迁移能力，为语言模型代理查询知识图谱提供了一种更灵活、更通用的路径。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [54] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBO-Merge框架，将模型合并参数选择形式化为黑箱多目标优化问题，使用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下逼近Pareto前沿。在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上的指令-数学及指令-数学-代码场景中，使用Linear、SLERP、TIES和块级合并算子进行验证，MOBO-Merge在12项比较中有11项获得比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并直接在权重空间中组合已训练模型，是计算高效的替代微调方案。但合并参数的选择十分困难：下游评估成本高昂、梯度不可用、源模型能力可能相互冲突。现有方法缺乏系统性的参数搜索机制。

Method: 将合并参数选择建模为黑箱多目标优化问题，引入MOBO-Merge框架，该框架与具体合并算子无关，采用多目标贝叶斯优化逼近Pareto前沿，在有限评估预算下工作。在两种场景（双模型指令-数学和三模型指令-数学-代码）中使用Linear、SLERP、TIES和块级合并算子进行实证评估。

Result: 在保留的基准测试划分上，MOBO-Merge在12项报告比较中有11项获得比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。一维Linear插值的增益较小，但在TIES、块级和三目标搜索中增益显著更大。没有合并算子普遍最佳：TIES在四个家族-设置组合中的三个占优，而Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中表现最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达力更强的合并参数化的搜索层是有价值的，MOBO-Merge为模型合并的参数选择提供了高效且有效的系统化框架。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [55] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种新的凸损失函数，用于SVM和神经网络的二分类任务。通过粒子群优化算法求解原始SVM问题，并在多个小规模数据集上验证。实验表明新损失函数是标准损失的推广，但嵌套交叉验证结果显示使用与不使用新损失的泛化性能没有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过将模式相关性（pattern correlations）引入损失函数，探索是否能够提升二分类任务中SVM和神经网络的泛化性能。虽然在双SVM模型中存在实际限制，但在原始SVM形式和神经网络中可以应用这些新损失。

Method: 提出多个新的凸损失函数，作为标准损失的推广。使用粒子群优化（PSO）算法求解具有修改后损失的原始SVM问题，并在多个小规模数据集上进行实验。采用嵌套交叉验证（Nested Cross-Validation）程序来评估每个损失的泛化性能。

Result: 实验表明，新的损失函数在理论上可能增强某些数据集的泛化性能，但嵌套交叉验证的结果显示，使用与不使用新损失的泛化度量结果相同，即没有统计上的显著提升。

Conclusion: 尽管在损失函数中引入模式相关性在理论上具有提升泛化性能的潜力，但初步实验结果并未证实这一假设。该研究是一个初步探索，暗示在现有框架下新损失函数并未带来可衡量的实际性能改善。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [56] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeSIFT，一种无需威胁模型先验的检测方法，利用影响函数（influence functions）识别诱导大型语言模型生成不安全代码的提示批次，通过在三个开源代码LLM（3B-7B参数）上评估，AUROC最高达0.98，显著优于静态分析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM不安全代码生成的防御方法依赖预定义威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，难以应对新型攻击。需要一种不依赖特定攻击类别先验知识、能够检测诱导不安全代码生成的通用方法。

Method: CodeSIFT采用威胁模型无关（threat-model-agnostic）的检测框架：首先利用影响函数衡量生成代码对模型参数空间的参数影响，然后通过统计检验判断候选提示批次是否偏离良性参考分布，从而识别异常行为。同时引入两个涵盖多种漏洞类型的基准数据集用于评估。

Result: 在三个开源代码LLM（3B至7B参数规模）上，CodeSIFT在中等到高注入率下AUROC最高达0.98，保持校准良好的假阳性率，并显著超越静态分析基线。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测方法为识别恶意代码生成提示提供了有前景的方向，无需预先了解攻击类别即可有效检测，为LLM安全防御开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [57] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB）问题，首次给出了QMAB的minimax下界Ω(K log(T/K))和有限动作QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))下界，并通过基于设计的消除算法实现了有限动作QLB中与d线性相关的后悔度，将维度依赖从d²改进到d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对QMAB的后悔度为O(K log T)，对d维QLB的后悔度为O(d² polylog T)。一个悬而未决的问题是：K log T的量级是否不可避免，以及d²的依赖是否可以被改进。作者旨在解决Wan等人[2023]提出的这一问题，并确定是否存在与T无关的后悔度。

Method: 作者通过三个核心技术实现：1）利用多项式方法和三角多项式的Remez型不等式，证明了高置信度的单臂量子检测下界（区分固定奖励均值与区间备择假设）；2）通过bandit-to-testing归约将单臂下界提升为QMAB的minimax下界，并通过线性嵌入得到有限动作QLB的下界；3）提出基于设计的消除算法，将低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小幅G-最优设计结合，通过匹配设计权重的查询分配实现线性维度的后悔度。

Result: 证明了QMAB的minimax下界为Ω(K log(T/K))，有限动作QLB的下界为Ω(d log(T/d))。当动作集大小为poly(d)时，设计基于消除的算法后悔度与d线性相关，优于先前的d²复杂度，且与下界匹配至多对数因子。该算法进一步将维度依赖从d²降至d^(3/2)（使用Quantum Monte Carlo估计时），再通过低方差估计器使重建误差以方差方式聚合，消除了剩余的√d因子。

Conclusion: 本文首次确立了QMAB和有限动作QLB的minimax下界，回答了Wan等人关于是否存在与T无关后悔度的问题；同时提出设计性消除算法在有限动作场景下达到（至多对数因子）最优的线性d依赖，显著改进了先前的d²结果。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [58] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的多模态轨迹预测方法，通过构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表并使用乘积核检索邻居，在无需GPU和零学习参数的情况下达到与57M参数Transformer相当的精度，且在数据稀缺场景下性能显著优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的轨迹预测方法需要大量训练数据和GPU资源，在数据稀缺的新地理区域部署时性能严重退化，且训练成本高昂。作者希望开发一种无需GPU训练、零学习参数的方法，能够在数据稀缺条件下保持稳定性能。

Method: 构建历史"状态→下一位置"对的转移表；使用基于空间邻近度、方位角、速度和时序上下文的乘积核来检索最近邻。提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成覆盖不同合理路线的轨迹）和束搜索（寻找最大似然路径）。

Result: 在丹麦海事AIS基准（TrAISformer）上，该方法在完整数据下达到与57M参数Transformer相当的精度，且在数据稀缺场景（低至10%训练数据）下大幅优于Transformer，后者此时性能严重退化。

Conclusion: 该方法可基于少一个数量级的历史数据在新地理区域部署，且无需GPU训练，为轨迹预测提供了一种高效、鲁棒且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [59] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了业务流程中延迟检测的固有难度。基于14个事件日志，发现剩余时间分布通常严重右偏（仅少数案例存在大延迟），现有模型能很好地捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现不佳。进一步发现预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加（异方差性），且延迟案例的较高不确定性而非类别不平衡才是根本问题。基于该相关性，作者提出了利用不确定性来改善延迟案例识别的方法，并指出不确定性感知建模是未来PPM研究的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测性流程监控（PPM）在剩余时间预测方面已通过深度学习架构取得了显著进展，但延迟检测本身的内在难度鲜为人知。现有评估通常使用聚合指标，无法揭示模型在目标分布上的表现差异，尤其是对运营最关键的大延迟案例。这一空白阻碍了对延迟检测困难的深入理解，也限制了针对性改进方法的发展。

Method: 作者在14个事件日志上进行实证分析，首先检验剩余时间分布的偏度特征；其次评估现有模型在分布不同区域（特别是高延迟尾部）的表现；接着通过分析预测不确定性随延迟幅度的变化揭示异方差性；最后基于不确定性-延迟相关性提出改进延迟案例识别的方法，并评估了多种缓解类别不平衡问题的策略。

Result: 研究发现：（1）剩余时间分布通常强烈右偏，仅小部分案例存在大延迟；（2）现有模型能很好捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现差；（3）存在显著的异方差性，预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加；（4）缓解不平衡问题的方法收益有限，表明根本问题可能是不确定性而非不平衡；（5）利用不确定性-延迟相关性可显著改善延迟案例的识别。

Conclusion: 延迟检测的核心难度来源于延迟案例固有的更高不确定性，而非简单的类别不平衡。不确定性-延迟的相关性可被利用以提升延迟案例识别性能，不确定性感知建模应成为未来PPM研究的重要方向。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [60] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"imposter"判别式自监督任务，通过用其他实体的真实观测替换实体的部分特征并训练编码器识别被替换的特征，从而迫使模型学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。在ERA5-Land再分析数据上对比多种自监督目标，发现最优pretext task取决于下游任务类型，且imposter能与现有SSL目标互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中实体的特征通常同时受到物理定律的约束，但现有自监督学习目标大多忽略了这种物理一致性。作者希望利用物理一致性作为自监督信号，让模型学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。

Method: 提出"imposter"判别式预文本任务：从一个实体中取子集特征，用另一个实体捐赠的（个体上合理的）真实观测替换之，训练编码器识别哪些特征被替换。由于每个替换值单独来看都是合理的，任务只有在模型学会跨特征物理依赖时才能被解决。

Result: 在ERA5-Land全球再分析数据的21个环境变量上评估，覆盖7个下游任务（气候分类、碳通量估算、径流预测等）。发现最有效的预文本任务取决于下游任务家族而非单一目标的普适优越性；imposter与现有SSL目标组合时提供互补信息。研究还包含首个在共享架构和预训练预算下对陆地表面建模SSL目标的系统性比较。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的新型自监督来源，imposter任务能够有效学习跨特征物理依赖，并为现有自监督框架提供互补增益。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [61] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为数据增强之外的一种等变性增强技术，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下SWA能在增强数据上提供超出单纯性能提升的等变性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是将学习任务的对称性融入深度网络的有效方式，但无限大的深度集成虽然能实现完美对称性，却需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。作者希望寻找一种不需要重复训练、成本更低的替代性集成技术来获得等变性增益。

Method: 作者将训练结束时的随机训练轨迹近似为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程来理论分析SWA。在无限宽度极限下，证明SWA结合数据增强可以提供超出单纯SWA性能提升的等变性增强效果，并在计算机视觉和图分类等多个模型上进行了广泛数值实验验证。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供了超出SWA本身性能提升预期之外的等变性增强。这一结果在涵盖离散和连续对称性的计算机视觉与图分类任务的多个模型上均得到实验验证。

Conclusion: SWA作为集成技术不需要重复训练，是一种在增强数据上获得等变性增益的划算替代方案，其效果在理论上（无限宽度极限）和实验上均得到支持。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [62] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeaMoE是一种面向解码高效性的MoE架构，通过将专家按专业领域分组共享大部分参数（"部门"内共享参数+少量私有参数），并结合两阶段路由策略避免冗余权重加载，在小批量解码场景下显著减少专家权重装载量和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型被广泛用于实时交互应用（如编码助手、实时音视频交互），这些场景要求极低响应延迟，常采用小批量解码。在小批量解码下MoE推理变为内存受限，主要瓶颈是专家权重加载。现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩、预训练阶段细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销。

Method: DeaMoE将专家划分为若干"部门"（department），同一部门内的专家因来自相同专业领域而共享大部分参数，每个专家仅保留少量私有参数以体现独特性。此外设计了定制化两阶段路由策略来避免冗余加载。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE将每步加载权重减少高达50.9%，在A40上对7B预训练模型实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速；在微基准测试中，对DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别实现最高2.00倍和1.97倍的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过在架构层面设计专家参数共享和两阶段路由策略，有效缓解了小批量解码下MoE推理的专家权重加载瓶颈，在不牺牲精度且不引入额外开销的前提下显著提升了解码效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CytoBERT是一个基于自监督预训练的开源基础模型，专门用于处理具有可变标记面板的单细胞流式细胞术数据，通过大规模异质数据集的预训练实现跨研究的迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞术数据因实验方案和特征选择的不同而高度异质且缺乏标准化，现有机器学习方法难以跨研究应用和迁移。虽然基础模型在其他领域取得进展，但在细胞术领域相关方法仍然稀缺。

Method: CytoBERT在包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞的语料库上进行自监督预训练，通过标记标准化处理异质的标记面板，学习细胞内部可迁移的标记间关系。预训练后针对样本级分类任务进行微调。

Result: 在样本级分类任务上，微调后的CytoBERT证明了跨异质细胞术数据集的迁移学习是可行的，为可扩展、泛化的细胞术分析提供了基础。

Conclusion: CytoBERT作为首个针对细胞术数据的基础模型，通过标记标准化和自监督预训练解决了数据异质性问题，验证了跨数据集迁移学习的可行性，为细胞术分析提供了可扩展的通用起点。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [64] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Power Sampling的一个悖论：它虽然能将概率质量集中于正确轨迹，却可能使下游推理（如self-consistency）性能下降高达18.5个百分点。作者将其归因于剂量不匹配（固定指数导致不同问题间分布变化差异大）和覆盖不匹配（全局锐化压缩了推理路径的多样性）。为此提出一种形变控制、支持保留的Power目标，在校准锐化强度的同时保留中等概率路径，在相同预算下逆转性能损失并超越标准多采样推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种免验证器的推理增强方法，展示了提升推理能力和作为通用前端的潜力，但作者发现其存在悖论：在向正确轨迹集中概率质量的同时反而损害下游推理性能。理解并修复这一矛盾是提升基于采样的推理方法效率的关键。

Method: 作者首先通过实验揭示Power Sampling在self-consistency等下游推理中造成性能下降（最高18.5个百分点），归因于两类不匹配：(1) 剂量不匹配——固定指数对不同问题产生的分布变化幅度差异巨大；(2) 覆盖不匹配——全局锐化将质量集中在少数主导路径上，即使pass@k较高也丧失了下游聚合所需的广泛推理路径支持。据此提出变形控制的支持保留Power目标，替代统一轨迹指数化，跨问题校准锐化强度并限制中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同预算的加权self-consistency实验中，修复后的采样器不仅逆转了全局Power造成的性能损失，还在多个推理基准上优于标准多采样推理方法。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的能力与其对下游任务的不良影响之间并非不可调和。通过识别并解决剂量和覆盖两类不匹配，可以实现既保留分布锐化优势又维持推理路径多样性的采样策略，从而更有效地服务下游推理任务。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [65] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过三种可扩展的连续松弛公式（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA）替代离散的神经元和激活层决策，适用于MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上实现了紧凑架构与竞争力的预测性能，并显著优于vanilla DARTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS本质上是一个双层优化问题，但由于离散的架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及候选架构训练的高昂成本，导致计算开销极大。现有方法难以高效地在连续空间中优化架构，亟需一种可扩展的通用框架来降低搜索成本。

Method: 提出了三种可扩展的连续松弛公式：(1) NAS-NG（基于神经元门控），用连续门控替代离散的神经元选择；(2) NAS-MA（基于混合激活），用连续混合替代离散的激活函数选择；(3) NAS-NGMA（结合神经元门控与混合激活），同时松弛两类决策。这些松弛使原本组合性的架构空间可进行可微优化，从而在双层优化框架下高效求解。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试准确率，NAS-NG仅以0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%准确率。在CIFAR-10上持续优于vanilla DARTS。NAS-NG还能优化过度参数化及文献最优架构，在减少参数的同时提升准确率。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化被证明是离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为跨多种架构的高效神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [66] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一个基于线性规划的可微神经架构搜索框架，通过将验证损失梯度与训练损失Hessian构建线性规划来计算架构更新方向，在保持模型参数最优性的同时改善泛化性能。在DARTS搜索空间上实现S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS两种变体，在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet上均优于标准DARTS及其他变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微NAS将架构搜索空间松弛到连续域后，可以应用连续优化原理，但现有方法未能充分利用数学规划工具来同时优化架构和保证模型参数最优性。作者希望提出一个数学规划框架，高效地计算架构更新方向，提高搜索效率和泛化性能。

Method: LP-NAS将可微NAS问题形式化为线性规划（LP）：利用验证损失梯度编码泛化目标，利用训练损失Hessian约束模型参数的最优性条件，通过求解LP得到架构更新方向。基于该LP下降方向高效导航架构搜索空间。提出了两种计算高效的变体S-LP-NAS（简化版）和R-LP-NAS（正则化版），并分别在DARTS搜索空间上实现了S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS。

Result: LP-DARTS在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上均优于标准DARTS，在搜索和评估阶段都表现更好；早期搜索迭代中收敛更快且验证性能显著更高；与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS等变体对比也展现了有效性；发现的架构在ImageNet上验证了可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的数学编程框架能够为可微NAS提供高效且有效的架构优化途径，实现更快的收敛和更好的泛化性能，同时具有广泛的适用性和良好的架构可迁移性。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [67] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了sMuon（small Muon）优化器，通过线性化和最小二乘近似解决Muon优化器无法直接应用于LoRA低秩参数化微调的问题，仅使用矩阵乘法实现高效计算，在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中展现出适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中表现优于其他优化器，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中较少使用，主要原因是LoRA的低秩参数化无法进行数学上的正交化操作，导致Muon无法自然地与LoRA结合。本文旨在解决这一不兼容性问题。

Method: 通过将松弛的Muon目标函数在低秩设置下进行线性化，然后利用最小二乘法近似求解。实现上仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）操作，避免了复杂的线性代数分解例程，从而实现了高效的sMuon优化器。

Result: sMuon在监督微调（SFT）和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好。虽然结果因模型和评估任务而异，但总体而言，将Muon用于低秩微调可提供适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功地将Muon优化器的优势扩展到了低秩微调场景，通过数学近似和高效实现解决了Muon与LoRA的组合难题，为PEFT领域提供了一种新的优化器选择。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [68] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨表格数据生成（CTDG）的两阶段框架。第一阶段将异构原始表格转换为统一列结构的统计表格，捕获边际分布和成对相关性；第二阶段使用扩散Transformer模型学习这些同构统计表格的结构模式并生成新的统计表格，再通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建原始形态的合成表格。实验验证了该方法在统计表示保真度和合成数据保真度-多样性权衡上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据生成方法局限于单输入表格场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格。需要一种能够从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段将每个异构原始表转换为标准化统计表（统一列集），捕获原始列的边际分布和列间的成对相关性；(2) 第二阶段使用扩散Transformer模型在统一的统计表上学习结构模式并生成合成统计表；(3) 通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换从生成的统计表重建合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在学习的统计表示上具有较高保真度，在生成合成数据的保真度-多样性权衡上表现良好，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异构表格中学习统一的生成模型，支持生成无限数量的逼真合成异构表格，弥补了现有方法在多表格场景下的不足。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [69] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollplex，一个针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）强化学习后训练的高效运行时系统。通过将参考模型与训练阶段的前缀计算分解并重叠到rollout解码窗口内，同时引入阶段感知的显存管理和并行度感知的权重重用机制，在32块H800 GPU上相比串行共置方案获得1.23–1.30倍加速，相比同GPU预算下的解耦方案获得1.57–2.24倍加速，且保持同步on-policy RL语义不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy RL运行时采用严格的串行阶段执行（rollout、reference scoring、actor training），这对纯文本RL有效，但对VLM而言是浪费的。因为VLM处理密集视频输入和prompt前缀占各阶段大量计算，而这些前缀处理与生成响应无关，可以在rollout解码窗口内并行执行，否则GPU计算能力被闲置。此外，朴素共置方案显存超出预算（Qwen2.5-VL-32B需约165 GiB/GPU），且rollout与训练偏好不同的张量并行（TP）度和权重布局，直接并发执行在工程上不可行。

Method: Rollplex通过两个核心机制实现前缀计算与rollout解码的重叠：（1）阶段感知的显存管理——依据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留，在rollout解码窗口内调度参考与训练阶段的前缀计算；（2）并行度感知的权重重用——对TP度不同但布局兼容的张量共享同一物理存储，仅对不兼容的张量进行重建，避免维护完整的第二份actor权重副本。整个调度保持同步on-policy RL更新语义。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相对于串行共置方案获得1.23–1.30倍加速，相对同GPU预算下的解耦（disaggregation）方案获得1.57–2.24倍加速，同时保持同步RL更新的正确性。仅朴素并发内核启动（Qwen2.5-VL-32B）就需约165 GiB每GPU显存，而Rollplex通过其机制成功规避了这一资源瓶颈。

Conclusion: Rollplex证明了一个关键洞察：VLM的RL后训练中，与生成无关的密集前缀计算可以被调度到rollout解码窗口内并行执行，从而在不破坏同步on-policy语义的前提下大幅利用闲置GPU算力。通过巧妙的显存生命周期管理与跨TP度权重重用，该系统在严格资源约束下实现了显著的端到端加速，为VLM上的大规模RL后训练提供了新的系统设计范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [70] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RecipeNet，一种层级Transformer架构，用于配方（recipe）数据的表示学习。通过在步骤内编码字段级交互和跨步骤编码顺序依赖，显著优于现有表格学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据在材料合成、药物制剂和工业制造等领域广泛存在，其过程表示为包含异构结构化字段的有序步骤序列。现有表格学习方法通常将此类结构扁平化为固定模式表示，无法捕捉层次化的字段交互和过程依赖关系。

Method: RecipeNet采用层级Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器捕获每个步骤内部字段级别的交互，上层Transformer编码器捕获步骤之间的顺序依赖关系，通过堆叠的编码器实现层次化建模。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet持续优于现有表格模型，验证了层级和顺序建模对配方表示学习的价值。

Conclusion: 层次化与序列化建模对于配方数据的表示学习至关重要，RecipeNet为处理结构化过程数据提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

### [71] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种改进的多输入多输出物理信息深度算子网络（M3PI-DeepONet），用于预测腹主动脉瘤（AAA）理想化几何结构中的非定常3D血流。通过聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）策略和逐层门控机制，该模型仅需0.3%的标注内部数据即可同时预测速度和压力场，相对L2速度误差低于4%、压力误差约5%，推理速度比参考CFD仿真快约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上可操作、患者特异性的血流动力学评估（如壁面剪切应力、涡结构和压力分布）对于判断腹主动脉瘤的风险演变至关重要。虽然PI-DeepONet在补充CFD工具方面显示出潜力，但在复杂3D流动中仍存在架构挑战，需要改进网络设计以提高预测精度和效率。

Method: 提出M3PI-DeepONet架构，核心包含两个创新点：(1) 聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）策略——将多个输入分支的潜在表示在注入主干网络之前进行融合，使坐标基适应多个物理约束；(2) 将逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑相结合，构建输入自适应的主干基函数。模型以3D Navier-Stokes方程作为物理定律约束，基于物理信息残差、初始条件和边界条件以及仅0.3%的标注内部数据（配合选定的分支条件信号）进行训练。

Result: M3PI-DeepONet同时预测非定常3D血流速度和压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；与参考CFD仿真相比，在分支输入信号可用的情况下，实现了约36倍的保守保留周期推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，为实时、无创的临床诊断迈出了重要一步，验证了通过网络拓扑创新（多分支+门控机制）可以显著提升物理信息算子网络在复杂3D血流预测中的性能。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [72] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了最大似然估计（MLE）在训练高斯过程（GP）用于概率回归/分类任务时的脆弱性，与理论上有充分依据的度量指标进行对比，并提出了实用解决方案。实验表明这些方案在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中有效，所构建的GP在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本方面甚至优于表格基础模型（如TabPFN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE是训练机器学习模型最常用且直观的方法，但其对假设条件非常敏感——当底层假设不成立时，模型会泛化很差。这种脆弱性甚至影响被广泛认为对过拟合非常鲁棒的高斯过程模型。作者希望从根本上评估MLE在训练GP用于工程设计中概率回归/分类任务时的脆弱性，并为实践者提供构建鲁棒GP的蓝图。

Method: 作者将理论上有充分依据的度量指标（替代MLE的方法）与MLE进行系统对比，评估其在训练GP时的行为差异，并在概率回归和分类任务上进行广泛实验。同时将这些方法应用于下游设计任务（如贝叶斯优化）来验证实际效果。

Result: 所提出的解决方案在概率回归、分类以及贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中均展现了有效性。构建出的GP在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本方面甚至优于表格基础模型（如TabPFN），说明MLE的替代方案可以显著提升GP的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为实践者提供了构建准确且鲁棒的高斯过程模型的具体蓝图，证明了在训练GP时采用理论上有依据的度量指标替代MLE可以带来显著性能提升，并且这些改进在工程设计的实际下游任务中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [73] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN深度学习框架，用于智能制造中的质量监控和故障隔离，基于inception残差神经网络构建控制图监控产品缺陷概率，利用迁移学习识别缺陷区域，并建立了极小极大最优收敛率，实证表明并非总是升级监控设备对制造商最有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造过程中大量传感器和成像设备的部署为质量监控提供了丰富数据，但现有方法在缺陷检测精度和故障定位能力上存在不足，且面临训练数据不足的现实挑战。作者希望将深度学习能力整合到工业质量控制实践中。

Method: 1) 基于inception残差神经网络架构构建控制图，监控产品含缺陷的可能性；2) 提出利用迁移学习的故障区域估计器来识别缺陷区域；3) 针对训练数据不足的情况，提出只需少量样本的迁移监控技术；4) 采用假设检验方法定量评估方法的适用性；5) 从理论上建立缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率。

Result: 理论层面建立了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率；实证层面通过模拟实验和真实数据展示了该方法相较于最先进方法的优越性能。此外，结果揭示了反直觉的管理启示：制造商并非总应不惜成本地持续升级监控设备。

Conclusion: MODERN框架为智能制造的工业质量控制提供了完整的深度学习方法，在理论上具有最优收敛保证，在实证中表现优于现有方法，并为设备升级决策提供了重要的管理启示。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.

</details>


### [74] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在Occam反演框架下系统梳理了l1正则化（总变差、融合lasso等）的数学形式，指出多维TV正则化存在方向不变性与非不变性两种形式，并证明在1D情况下合成与分析两种lasso融合路径等价。通过对比坐标下降、IRLS和Split Bregman三种求解器在线性与非线性地球物理问题中的表现，推荐1D问题使用坐标下降、2D问题使用IRLS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球物理中的l1正则化反演偏离了统计学和成像领域的成熟理论体系，存在多维TV正则化两种形式（方向不变与非不变）被混淆、求解器选择缺乏系统比较等问题，作者希望将l1正则化反演统一到广泛接受的Occam反演框架中，以促进其在地球物理学中的应用。

Method: 在Occam框架下，针对l2数据范数与l1模型正则化：（1）从数学上证明1D情形下合成与分析两种lasso融合路径等价；（2）将框架推广至多维并扩展至字典小波变换等算子；（3）推导并剖析坐标下降、IRLS和Split Bregman三种常用求解器的数学机制；（4）在一系列线性和非线性问题（1D回归、2D去模糊、1D航空瞬变电磁法）上对比三种求解器，包括野外实测数据验证。

Result: 证明了1D中合成/分析lasso融合等价性；明确了多维TV两种形式的方向不变性差异；在测试问题上，坐标下降适用于1D问题，IRLS比Split Bregman更优，所需最小二乘求解次数少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将l1正则化反演的多种形式统一呈现为熟悉的Occam反演框架，为地球物理学家提供清晰的求解器选择指南（1D用坐标下降、2D用IRLS），有望推动l1正则化反演在地球物理学中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem. We extend the framework to multiple dimensions and to operators such as wavelet transforms in a dictionary, unravelling the mathematics behind three commonly used solvers for l1 regularised problems. These are coordinate descent, iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS), and split Bregman. We use them to solve a sequence of problems that are linear (1D regression, 2D deblurring) and nonlinear (1D airborne transient electromagnetics) including a field data example. We recommend coordinate descent for 1D problems, and IRLS over split Bregman for 2D problems, with IRLS requiring up to an order of magnitude fewer least squares solves. We hope this work will further encourage geophysicists to adopt l1 regularised inversion, by presenting its various forms as a familiar Occam's inversion.

</details>


### [75] [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)
*Xiaohong Chen,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Chen Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离线强化学习框架，将算子估计与价值函数学习解耦，利用条件扩散模型估计奖励函数和转移核，从而构造数据驱动的近似最优Bellman算子，并通过最小化经验Bellman残差来学习Q*的深度估计器。理论上给出了最优Bellman算子学习的非渐近收敛率、超额Bellman残差风险的oracle速率以及Q*估计器的L²收敛速率，且不依赖完备性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中估计最优动作价值函数Q*可归结为求解最优Bellman方程，但奖励函数和转移核未知，导致最优Bellman算子无法直接从数据观测。现有方法常依赖完备性假设，而该假设在深度RL理论中难以验证或不成立，亟需一种无需该假设的新框架。

Method: （1）将算子估计与价值函数学习解耦；（2）利用条件扩散模型估计奖励分布和转移核，构造数据驱动的近似最优Bellman算子；（3）将估计算子代入Bellman方程，在神经网络函数类上最小化经验Bellman残差获得Q*的深度估计器；（4）理论上通过端到端分析条件扩散估计在总变差距离下的收敛速率，并建立残差风险与L²收敛速率。

Result: 建立了三个关键理论结果：（1）最优Bellman算子学习的非渐近收敛速率（基于条件扩散估计的总变差距离端到端分析）；（2）超额Bellman残差风险的oracle速率Õ(n^{-2β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})；（3）在可集中性条件下，Q*深度估计器的L²收敛速率Õ(n^{-β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})。数值实验验证了方法的有效性和强经验表现。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了离线RL中Bellman算子不可观测的难题，无需完备性假设即可获得Q*估计器的收敛保证，为深度离线强化学习提供了新的理论视角和实用的算法方案。

Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis of conditional diffusion estimation in total variation distance. We then establish the oracle value-stage rate $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\frac{2β}{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the excess Bellman residual risk. Finally, under a concentrability condition, we translate this residual bound into an $L^2$ convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\fracβ{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the resulting deep estimator of $Q^*$, where $d_x$ and $d_a$ denote the dimensions of the state and action spaces, respectively, and $β$ denotes the Hölder smoothness index of $Q^*$. Importantly, our theoretical analysis does not rely on completeness assumptions commonly used in deep RL theory. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [76] [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)
*Yang Peng,Liangyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究固定策略下回报分布泛函的在线统计推断。作者使用非参数分布时序差分学习从单条马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布，证明了Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量的根号T误差在Cramér空间中弱收敛到中心化高斯随机元素，并建立了相应的bootstrap一致性，从而为光滑统计泛函（方差、CVaR、期望损失、expectiles）和非光滑统计泛函（分位数等）的推断提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中分布式时序差分（DTD）学习可用于估计回报分布，但针对单条马尔可夫轨迹下的分布估计，缺乏系统的在线统计推断理论。特别是如何对回报分布的统计泛函（如方差、CVaR、分位数等）进行可靠的置信区间估计和假设检验，是一个尚未解决的关键问题。

Method: 采用非参数分布式时序差分学习从单条马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布。对Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量，利用Cramér空间中的弱收敛理论证明根号T误差的中心极限定理；通过bootstrap方法证明条件弱收敛到相同高斯极限，用于光滑泛函推断。对非光滑泛函，建立估计回报CDF在T^{-1/2}邻域内的局部渐近理论及其bootstrap类比，用于处理由CDF方程定义的非光滑泛函（如分位数）。

Result: 证明了Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量的根号T误差弱收敛到Cramér空间中的中心化高斯随机元素；bootstrap估计与原估计的根号T差异条件弱收敛到同一高斯极限。这些结果支持对光滑统计泛函（方差、CVaR、期望损失、expectiles）和非光滑统计泛函（分位数等）进行有效的bootstrap推断。

Conclusion: 本文为固定策略下回报分布泛函的在线统计推断提供了严格的理论基础，通过Cramér空间中的弱收敛和非光滑泛函的局部渐近理论，实现了对多种回报分布统计量的有效bootstrap推断，填补了分布式TD学习在统计推断维度上的理论空白。

Abstract: We study online statistical inference for functionals of the return distribution under a fixed policy. The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cramér space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. This theory allows us to conduct inference for nonsmooth statistical functionals characterized by CDF equations, including return quantiles.

</details>

### [77] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricForge，通过反思式演化从少量带标签轨迹中自动生成可读的评判规则文本，用于替代昂贵的环境奖励作为语言模型智能体的自动评判器。与手工编写规则（G-Eval）或微调评判器权重不同，RubricForge将规则锚定在真实结果上，具有可归因性。实验表明，其原始一致性与G-Eval差异不显著，但在更重要的部署相关指标——假通过率上约降低一半（0.115 vs 0.173），且评判排序更忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时，依赖第二个语言模型作为自动评判器已成为常态，但此类评判器要么手工编写评分规则（难保正确性），要么微调权重（倾向给流畅但失败的轨迹过高评价）。核心问题是评判器是否可信，尤其是假通过（将失败轨迹判为成功）带来的部署风险远高于假失败。

Method: RubricForge从少量具有环境奖励真值标注的轨迹出发，通过反思式演化（reflective evolution）迭代优化评判规则文本，目标是最大化与真实环境奖励的一致性；随后冻结该规则，在单次模型调用中对留出轨迹进行评判，全程无需访问环境。输出规则为人类可读文本，每个判定均可归属到具名标准。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为智能体和评判器，在tau-bench（220条轨迹中取173条标注）和WebShop（160条）上：原始一致性与G-Eval差异不显著（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略逊于通用评判器（误差差-0.048，p=2x10^-4）；但tau-bench上假通过率约减半（0.115 vs 0.173，三次过度信任捕获且零逆转），WebShop排序更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励信号的评判器，假通过率而非总体一致性才是部署相关的关键指标——假通过会让有缺陷的智能体上线，而假失败仅损失一次重试成本。RubricForge的核心增益在于评判的忠实性（faithfulness），而非原始一致性或分数校准。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [78] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B多专家（MoE）模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上进行了系统的逐层敏感性分析。核心发现是层敏感性具有强深度依赖性：早期层（0-9）和中层（10-29）对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其是极晚期层（35-39）能容忍对低幅值专家的大规模掩蔽。晚期聚焦策略在300-prompt规模下保留249-255/300的Good+Similar输出（掩蔽640-1,145个专家），而平坦全层30%掩蔽仅保留150/300。在500-prompt验证切片上，极晚期窄幅策略（层35-39 @ 50%）达到最强质量/掩蔽专家权衡（419/500，仅掩蔽10,240个专家中的640个）。此外，将top-k路由从8降至6个活跃专家/令牌在100-prompt探针上显示显著墙钟时间减少且无Good+Similar损失，但尚未能与激进专家掩蔽干净地组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE架构在LLM中被广泛采用，但单个MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其在模型压缩场景下。理解各层对专家剪枝（掩蔽）的敏感性差异，是设计深度感知、高效MoE压缩策略的基础。

Method: 采用基于幅值的专家掩蔽方法，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上进行多阶段系统研究。评估覆盖100、300、500个prompt三种规模，运行于三台H100 GPU服务器。比较平坦全层掩蔽（30%）与按深度分组的聚焦掩蔽策略，并额外探究top-k路由宽度从8降至6的影响。

Result: （1）层敏感性呈深度依赖性：早期层（0-9）和中层（10-29）对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，晚期层（30-39）尤其极晚期层（35-39）可容忍激进的低幅值专家掩蔽。（2）平坦全层30%掩蔽仅保留150/300 Good+Similar输出（300-prompt规模），而晚期聚焦策略保留249-255/300，同时掩蔽640-1,145个专家。（3）在500-prompt验证切片上，极晚期策略（层35-39 @ 50%）以仅掩蔽640/10,240个专家实现419/500 Good+Similar，取得最优权衡。（4）top-k从8降至6在100-prompt探针上带来显著墙钟时间减少且无质量损失，但未与激进专家掩蔽干净组合。

Conclusion: 这些发现为深度感知的MoE专家掩蔽提供了实证基础，并建立了通向物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于训练的恢复的实际路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [79] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析研究46个任务、4个认知领域，发现大型语言模型（LLM）发展出与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：人类大脑中使用相同网络的任务在LLM中激活重叠的神经元，而使用不同网络的任务则激活不同的神经元。这种收敛的模块化涌现表明模块性可能是智能系统的根本属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类大脑的功能模块化组织（语言、形式推理、心理推理、物理推理各自独立网络）究竟是基于智能系统的必然设计原则，还是生物大脑演化过程中的偶然产物。这一问题的答案对于理解智能的本质以及AI系统设计具有深层意义。

Method: 对N=46个跨越四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务进行电路分析（circuit analyses），考察LLM内部神经元在不同类型任务中的激活重叠模式，并与人类大脑的网络组织进行对比。

Result: 研究发现LLM中涌现出镜像人类大脑的模块化架构：人类中调用相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元集合，而人类中调用不同网络的任务则在LLM中激活截然不同的神经元群体，表明模块化组织跨物种和跨系统（生物与人工）收敛出现。

Conclusion: 模块化架构在生物大脑和人工神经网络中独立且趋同地涌现，提示功能模块化可能是通用智能系统的基本属性，而非生物演化中的偶然事件。这为理解智能组织原理和指导AI架构设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons.
