Despite promising results, most existing approaches operate at the gene level without leveraging established transcriptomic modeling practices and rely on heterogeneous gene selection strategies, which complicates fair comparison across methods.
  We propose to reformulate morphology-to-transcriptomics prediction as conditional generation in transcriptional program space, thereby exploiting coordinated transcriptional variation instead of predicting genes independently. Using consensus non-negative matrix factorization (cNMF), we extract a low-dimensional set of transcriptional programs capturing coordinated expression variation in the training data, and train a conditional diffusion model to generate program activations from histology. This formulation exploits coordinated transcriptional variation and substantially lowers the dimensionality of the conditional generative task.

</details>


### [148] [Clearing the Fog: Towards Installing and Refining Proactive Exploration Capabilities in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339)
*Zhizhao Guan,Chen Huang,Ziming Liu,Hongru Liang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Tat-Seng Chua,Anthony G Cohn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM代理的主动探索能力（即通过探索环境获取信息以改进未来决策）。作者识别出阻碍该能力的两个根本瓶颈，并提出名为SAFARI的新方法，包含探索性数据构建（合成富含探索轨迹以缓解标准演示的后见之明偏差）和基于对比信号引导的强化学习优化（利用对比轨迹对区分有效探索与冗余游荡）。大量实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在现实环境中需要主动探索以获取信息来改善未来决策，然而现有方法缺乏对这种主动探索能力的系统研究与优化，且标准演示数据存在后见之明偏差，限制了代理的探索学习效果。

Method: SAFARI由两个组件构成：(1) 探索性数据构建——合成富含探索行为的轨迹，以缓解标准演示数据中的后见之明偏差；(2) 带对比信号引导的强化学习优化——利用对比轨迹对来区分哪些探索是有效的、哪些是冗余的游荡，从而引导模型学习高质量的探索策略。

Result: 大量实验表明SAFARI方法在增强LLM代理主动探索能力方面表现优异，并为主动探索的特性提供了深入见解。

Conclusion: SAFARI通过数据构建和对比信号引导的RL优化两个核心组件，有效解决了LLM代理主动探索中的两个瓶颈问题，实验验证了其有效性，揭示了主动探索的关键特征，并开源了代码。

Abstract: We study proactive exploration in LLM agents, i.e., the ability to explore an environment to acquire information that improves future decision-making. In this regard, we first identify two fundamental bottlenecks that hinder this capability and then propose \ours, a novel method designed to instill and refine proactive exploration. Specifically, \ours\ consists of two components: (1) Exploratory Data Construction, which synthesizes exploration-rich trajectories to mitigate the hindsight bias of standard demonstrations; and (2) RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance, which leverages contrastive trajectory pairs to distinguish productive exploration from redundant wandering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of \ours\ and provide insights into the characteristics of proactive exploration. Our code is available at: https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI.

</details>


### [149] [ScienceFlow: A long-horizon agent for ML research, scientific discovery and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14354)
*Mingming Zhao,Jiqian Dong,Kangping Xu,Zadid Hasan,Chengrui Fan,Shan Jiang,Shuai Mao,Ting Lingya,Linyi Zou,Tailin Zhou,Yun Hin Chan,Wenkai Zhang,Zhanhong Zhou,Guowei Huang,Hongliang Li,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Yanhui Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceFlow是一个端到端自动研究代理框架，通过将长期研究组织为可恢复的可执行状态段，配合ESTRA状态转换机制和证据感知的执行控制器，实现了高效的持续研究和资源分配，在MLE-bench上达到70.22%的Any-Medal分数，较先前最优结果提升4.92个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动研究代理在长时间视野下缺乏连续性、无法从死胡同中恢复、缺少价值驱动的计算资源分配机制，导致搜索效率低下、计算资源浪费且最终成功率降低。

Method: ScienceFlow将长期研究工作组织为基于可执行工作区的研究片段，将研究进度表示为可恢复的可执行状态。片段间转换由ESTRA（通过重新锚定的可执行状态转换）控制，选择活跃状态或存档状态作为下一锚点，决定继续或重定向研究轨迹。证据感知的执行控制器根据资源可用性、剩余预算和已验证进展分配物理作业资源。

Result: 在机器学习、科学建模和数学优化等任务上评估，在24小时预算内于完整MLE-bench上取得70.22%的Any-Medal分数（SOTA），较先前报告结果提升4.92个百分点。

Conclusion: 高效的状态管理、自适应探索和目标对齐的执行对于将自主研究扩展到超出短时视野的规模至关重要。

Abstract: Enabling LLM agents to sustain productive, stable, and goal-aligned research over extended horizons is a central challenge for autonomous machine learning and scientific discovery, as progress hinges on continuously managing evolving state, exploration decisions, and computational resources. Pioneering autoresearch agents, despite great success, still lack mechanisms for continuity, recovery from dead ends, and value-driven compute allocation, which inherently undermines overall search efficiency, wastes computational resources, and lowers the chance of ultimate success. To bridge this gap, we introduce ScienceFlow, an end-to-end autoresearch agent framework that organizes long-horizon research work into research segments grounded in executable workspaces. It represents research progress as recoverable executable states, enabling efficient exploration, revision, and execution. Transitions between research segments are governed by Executable-State Transition through Re-Anchoring (ESTRA), which selects either the live state or an archived state as the next anchor and determines whether to continue or redirect the research trajectory. An evidence-aware execution controller allocates resources to physical jobs based on resource availability, remaining budget, and validated progress. We evaluate ScienceFlow on tasks spanning machine learning, scientific modeling, and mathematical optimization. Results on diverse long-horizon benchmarks demonstrate its ability to sustain effective research processes, highlighted by a SOTA 70.22 percent Any-Medal score on the full MLE-bench within a 24-hour budget, outperforming prior reported results by 4.92 percentage points. The efficacy of ScienceFlow further demonstrates that efficient state management, adaptive exploration, and objective-aligned execution are critical for scaling autonomous research beyond short-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [150] [Disentangled Shared Representations Improve Morpho-Transcriptomic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14355)
*Julian Ostermaier,Swann Ruyter,Reuben Dorent,Daniel Racoceanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了标准与解耦（disentangled）变体的VAE和对比学习方法在多模态空间转录组学表示学习中的表现，发现对比目标优于VAE，且显式解耦共享与模态特定信息可提升重建和探测指标，但增益取决于模型家族、任务方向和解耦强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学能够同时获取基因表达和组织形态数据，为实现共享形态-转录组结构的表示学习提供了机会。然而，标准多模态模型通常将所有模态压缩到一个共同的潜在空间，未能显式区分共享变异和模态特异的变异来源，可能限制下游任务的效用。因此，作者希望探究显式解耦共享和私有潜在成分是否能改善H&E图像与空间转录组配对数据的多模态表示学习。

Method: 作者在匹配的实验条件下，在两个癌症队列中比较了VAE类方法和对比学习方法，每种方法均包含标准变体和解耦变体。解耦设计将潜在空间拆分为共享成分（两模态共有）和私人成分（各模态特有）。表示质量通过跨模态重建、下游探测任务以及跨模态探测迁移三个维度进行评估。

Result: 实验揭示了两个主要趋势：其一，对比学习目标在下游探测性能上优于VAE类模型；其二，解耦变体总体上改善了所选的重建和探测指标，但改善幅度依赖于模型家族、具体任务、评估方向和解耦强度等条件。

Conclusion: 显式因子化共享与模态特定信息可以改善空间转录组学的多模态表示学习，同时该研究提供了一个对未来基础模型有用的评估框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables the simultaneous profiling of gene expression and tissue morphology, creating an opportunity to learn multimodal representations capturing shared morpho-transcriptomic structure. However, standard multimodal models often compress modalities into a common latent space without explicitly separating shared and modality-specific sources of variation, which may limit downstream utility. We investigate whether explicit disentanglement of shared and private latent components improves multimodal representation learning for paired Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and ST data. We compare VAE-based and contrastive approaches, each in standard and disentangled variants, across two cancer cohorts under matched experimental conditions. Representations are evaluated using cross-modal reconstruction, downstream probing and cross-modal probe transfer. The experiments suggest two main trends. First, contrastive objectives yield higher downstream probing performance than VAE-based models. Second, disentangled variants improve the selected reconstruction and probing metrics, although the gains depend on the model family, task, direction, and disentanglement strength. Overall, our results suggest that explicitly factorizing shared and modality-specific information can improve multimodal representation learning for spatial transcriptomics and provides a useful evaluation framework for future foundation models.

</details>


### [151] [Designing Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14359)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Fattah,Deepak Kanneganti,Aneesh Krishna,Mufti Mahmud,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SFLaaS框架，通过碳约束神经架构搜索（NAS）解决联邦学习即服务（FLaaS）环境中的可持续性问题，将消费者可持续性画像转化为可行架构区域，并联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLaaS消费者的可持续性约束给维持碳可行的联邦训练带来重大挑战，这些约束在硬碳限制下常导致消费者参与不可行和联邦训练不稳定。

Method: 1) 需求驱动搜索空间，将消费者可持续性画像转化为可行架构区域；2) 消费者级碳可行性估计机制，在动态碳条件下评估候选架构；3) 可持续消费者调度策略，自适应选择可行消费者并分配本地工作负载；4) 进化搜索策略，在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率。

Result: 在真实世界数据集和模拟环境上的实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SFLaaS通过将碳可行性融入架构搜索与调度全过程，能够在硬碳约束下维持消费者的参与和统计数据的覆盖，实现可持续的联邦学习服务。

Abstract: The sustainability constraints of FLaaS consumers pose significant challenges to maintaining carbon-feasible federated training in FLaaS environments. These constraints often lead to infeasible consumer participation and unstable federated training under hard carbon constraints. We propose a Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service (SFLaaS), a carbon- constrained Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for heteroge- neous sustainable constraints. We introduce a requirement-driven search space that transforms consumer sustainability profiles into a feasible architecture region before federated execution. We develop a consumer-level carbon feasibility estimation mechanism to evaluate candidate architectures under dynamic carbon conditions. We propose a sustainable con- sumer scheduling strategy that adaptively selects feasible consumers and allocates local workloads to preserve consumer participation and statistical data coverage. An evolutionary search strategy jointly optimised for predictive performance, consumer feasibility, and participation coverage under hard carbon constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets and a simulated environment demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [152] [Wrong but Useful: Trajectory Value Beyond Answer Correctness in Multi-Agent Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14375)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Cheng-Hau Yang,Weijian Zheng,Fernando Llorente,Xiaolong Ma,Xinyang Li,Eliu A. Huerta,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"多样假设审议"(DHD)协议，用于测量多智能体推理系统中每条消息的"轨迹价值"——即该消息是否存在对后续推理的贡献（帮助或损害），而非单纯依据其正确性来筛选。通过缓存并回放独立生成的消息，发现"错误但有帮助"的消息在所有基准和模型组合中普遍存在，表明答案正确性与消息价值并非等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体推理系统通常基于一致性、置信度或自动评分来过滤消息以决定最终答案，这些过滤方法隐含假设"可能正确的消息就值得保留"。然而，一个错误的答案可能包含有用的分解、约束或科学原理。这种对"正确性"与"价值"的混淆可能导致系统丢弃有帮助的推理信息，作者希望通过受控测量协议来区分这两个属性。

Method: 提出DHD（Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation）受控测量协议：缓存五条独立生成的消息，并使用相同的下游求解器（称为"整合器"）分别在这些消息可用与隐藏两种条件下回放推理。通过对比回放结果来测量每条消息的"轨迹价值"——即使消息可用是帮助还是损害后续推理。实验覆盖五个数学和科学基准、两个开源模型家族（gpt-oss-120b和gemma-4-31B-it），并对重复出现的效果进行统计显著性检验（p=0.0002），还设计了对可重复的"错误但有帮助"消息的聚焦干预实验。

Result: 在每一个基准-模型组合中都发现了"错误但有帮助"的消息。在改变最终正确性的错误答案消息中，每个模型有超过四成（40%）的变化是有帮助的。受控重复实验表明，可重复的消息效应不太可能仅由回放变异造成（p=0.0002）。聚焦干预发现：完整消息效果最佳，保留其推理比仅保留其答案保留更多成功；完整消息优势的来源仍然未知。在同一问题内，反复的轨迹价值证据能比单纯答案正确性更好地识别"保留或移除"的决策。

Conclusion: 答案正确性提供了信息，但并不决定轨迹价值。DHD协议测量了这一缺失的属性，并产生可复用的标签，用于训练智能体何时应"倾听"（关注）其他智能体的消息。这为多智能体系统的消息筛选机制提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning systems often use agreement, confidence, or automated scores to decide which messages should shape a final answer. Such filtering assumes that a message likely to be correct is also worth keeping. Yet a wrong answer can contain a useful decomposition, constraint, or scientific principle. We test this distinction with Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD), a controlled measurement protocol that caches five independently generated messages and replays the same downstream solver, called the integrator, with each message available or hidden. The replay comparison measures a message's trajectory value: whether making the message available helps or harms subsequent reasoning. Across five mathematics and science benchmarks and two openly available model families, gpt-oss-120b and gemma-4-31B-it, wrong-helpful messages appear in every benchmark-model combination. Among wrong-answer messages that change final correctness, more than four in ten changes are helpful in each model. Controlled repeats show that the number of repeatable message effects is unlikely to arise from replay variation alone (p=0.0002). A focused intervention on repeatable wrong-helpful messages finds that the complete message works best, while retaining its reasoning preserves more success than retaining only its answer; the source of the complete-message advantage remains open. Within the same problem, repeated trajectory-value evidence also identifies a better keep-or-remove choice than answer correctness alone. Answer correctness is therefore informative but does not determine trajectory value. DHD measures this missing property and produces reusable labels for learning when agents should listen.

</details>


### [153] [AgentRewind: Recoverable Execution for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14380)
*Yu Zhuang,Kefei Chen,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng,Jian Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRewind是一个运行时恢复框架，通过记录智能体上下文和受控环境的对齐检查点，使LLM智能体能够回退到早期状态并从之前尝试中恢复，从而提升长时程执行任务的成功率。同时构建了MettleBench基准来评估长时程工程任务的完成情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中LLM智能体早期发生的错误会通过上下文和环境状态传播，难以通过后续动作逆转。现有方法主要依赖计划细化和安全检查来减少错误，但错误发生后缺乏恢复支持。

Method: 提出AgentRewind框架，记录智能体上下文和受控环境的状态检查点，使智能体能够回退到较早状态并从之前的尝试中恢复执行。同时构建MettleBench基准，用于评估包含一系列相关需求的长时程工程任务中的任务完成度和部分进展。

Result: 实验覆盖多种任务、多个模型、执行策略和智能体框架，结果表明AgentRewind相比基线方法提升了任务成功率和平均检查清单进度。

Conclusion: AgentRewind通过在运行时提供状态回退与恢复机制，有效改善了LLM智能体在长时程任务中的表现，弥补了现有方法在错误发生后缺乏恢复支持的不足。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks require LLM agents to interact with their environments over long execution horizons. Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [154] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tripwire，一种无需训练的防御方法，通过FDR控制的逐神经元假设检验识别安全特异性神经元，并利用触发器式钳制（trigger-style clamp）将选中的神经元固定在有害条件均值激活上，从而在最小化模型效用损失的同时有效防御越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经元/路径层面的防御方法存在两大局限：(1) 抑制有毒神经元需要较大干预足迹，严重损害模型效用；(2) 基于外部分类器识别安全神经元的方法会误伤对模型效用重要的神经元。此外，现有方法始终开启，即使没有攻击也会扰动正常请求。因此需要一种既能精准识别安全特异性神经元、又能在无攻击时保持模型正常行为的防御方法。

Method: Tripwire包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过FDR（假发现率）控制的逐神经元假设检验，结合效用特异性过滤器，精准识别安全特异性神经元；(2) 采用触发器式钳制机制，将识别出的神经元固定在有害条件均值激活上，注入内部有害输入信号以触发对齐阶段学到的拒绝行为；(3) 提供两种可证明等价的部署模式——检测器门控的推理时干预（detector-gated inference-time intervention）和离线偏置补丁权重编辑（offline bias-patch weight edit）。

Result: 在四个安全对齐的LLM和四种代表性攻击上，Tripwire将平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，同时在MT-Bench上的效用损失仅为0.5%至5.3%，是所有防御方法中效用损失最小的。

Conclusion: Tripwire通过精准识别安全特异性神经元并结合触发式钳制机制，成功实现了在防御越狱攻击的同时最小化对模型效用的影响，且提供灵活的部署模式，为LLM安全防御提供了有效的训练无关方案。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [155] [LLMs Don't Pay for the Jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14397)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过马克斯·普朗克解决黑体辐射问题（提出E = hν）的历史案例，论证了大语言模型无法进行溯因推理的根本原因不在于缺乏具身模拟，而在于缺乏"知识论错误与物理代价之间的耦合机制"。作者形式化了"热力学耦合"概念，并指出固定权重的Transformer推理无论模型规模多大都缺乏这种耦合，这与经验证据（输出熵随因果难度几乎不变，而准确率从100%降至17%）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应关于LLM是否具备溯因推理能力的争论。Zahavy认为LLM无法进行爱因斯坦等价原理那样的溯因跳跃，并将其归因于缺乏具身模拟；而Zheng-Xin和Farmer质疑具身性是否是溯因的必要条件。本文通过普朗克案例提出第三种解释：溯因的真正前提既非具身模拟，而是某种更深层的物理机制。

Method: 采用概念分析与形式化结合的方法。首先论证普朗克提出E = hν既非归纳也非演绎的产物，指出其动机源于经典理论的一个数学推论（无穷大能量预测对应有限实测值）产生的知识论错误与物理代价之间的耦合。作者将这种耦合形式化为"热力学耦合"，并将其作为溯因推理的必要条件，进而分析固定权重Transformer推理是否具备此耦合。

Result: 形式化表明固定权重Transformer推理缺乏知识论错误与物理代价之间的热力学耦合，且该缺失与模型规模无关。经验结果支持这一结论：随因果难度增加，准确率从100%降至17%，但输出熵几乎不变，说明模型内部没有形成"错误即代价"的反馈机制。

Conclusion: 机器溯因缺失的成分可能比具身性更深层：一个系统必须具备某种物理机制，使知识论错误变得足够昂贵以迫使系统进行修正。这为构建具备溯因能力的AI提供了新的设计方向——关注代价耦合机制而非仅仅增加模型规模或引入具身模拟。

Abstract: Zahavy [2026] argues that Large Language Models, despite their capabilities in induction and deduction, cannot perform the abductive "Jump" that produced Einstein's equivalence principle, and attributes this limitation to the absence of embodied simulation. Zheng-Xin [2026] and Farmer [2026] question whether embodiment is necessary for abduction, pointing to alternative routes to General Relativity and forms of abduction that require no sensorimotor grounding. Max Planck resolved the blackbody radiation problem in 1900. Planck's move to E = hν required no embodied simulation. It was motivated by a mathematical consequence of classical theory, an infinite predicted energy for a finite measured quantity, that could not be physically accepted. We show that neither induction nor deduction could have produced the postulate and argue that its adoption required a coupling between epistemic error and physical cost. We formalize this distinction through thermodynamic coupling and show that fixed-weight transformer inference lacks such coupling, regardless of model scale. This is consistent with empirical results showing that output entropy remains nearly unchanged across tasks with sharply increasing causal difficulty, even as accuracy falls from 100% to 17%. We therefore argue that the missing ingredient in machine abduction may lie deeper than embodiment: a system must have some physical mechanism through which epistemic error becomes costly enough to force revision.

</details>


### [156] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文概述了AI科学家（AI Scientists）的过去与未来——即能够自动化科学发现全流程的机器系统。文章回顾了Adam（首个通过假设形成与物理实验循环做出新颖科学发现的机器）和Eve（现代自动驾驶实验室架构的建立者），指出当前基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人已使构建远超人机能力的系统成为可能。文章强调核心问题不再是单个科学组件的自动化，而是集成问题——AI科学家需将神经学习与逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、仿真、实验设计、机器人技术和形式化科学记录相结合。AI科学家有望使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化、更可重复，并能探索人类难以企及的复杂系统。最后介绍了Nobel Turing Challenge（目标2050年开发能自动化诺贝尔级发现的AI系统），目前进展已超前于计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术在科学各环节（假设生成、实验设计、数据分析等）的自动化已逐步实现，作者认为当下关键问题已从"单个组件能否自动化"转变为"如何将各组件集成为统一的科学代理系统"。作者旨在通过回顾Adam和Eve的历史经验，阐明AI科学家研究的现状与未来方向，激励研究社区朝着全自动科学发现系统的目标前进。

Method: 本文为综述性文章（survey），通过对AI科学家领域的历史发展进行梳理——从Adam（假设生成+物理实验循环）到Eve（自动驾驶实验室架构），再到当前由基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人驱动的新一代系统——分析技术演进脉络，并指出集成（integration）是当前面临的核心技术挑战。文章还以Nobel Turing Challenge为远景框架，评估了该领域的发展进度与预期目标。

Result: 文章指出：单一科学环节的自动化（假设生成、实验设计、数据分析等）已被证明可行；真正瓶颈在于跨组件的系统集成。同时，Nobel Turing Challenge设定的2050年目标目前进展超前于时间表，说明AI科学家的可行性正逐步被实践验证。

Conclusion: AI科学家具有变革科学的巨大潜力——能够使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化和更可重复，能探索超出人类能力的复杂系统，并使数千个AI科学家协同解决单一问题。当这一愿景实现时，将创造新的科学形态并深刻改变世界。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [157] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了optstop，一种基于精度的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序贯测量问题，在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计精确或稳定时停止。在模拟实验中可节省57%-97%的试验量，同时保证结论等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM评估使用固定采样预算，对每个测试项进行相同次数的采样，即使在估计已经精确之后仍继续采样，浪费了大量计算资源。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断，构建自适应停止框架optstop，支持二元、有序和连续型结果变量；每个基准项保持可采样资格，无需校准题库；支持在线运行或回顾性运行；当测量性能接近零时采用更谨慎的采样策略作为安全保障。

Result: 在200项、10个epoch的示例评估中，跨9个验证设置移除了57%-97%的计划试验量，同时总体结论与完整运行等价。

Conclusion: LLM评估计算资源可以根据不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计的具体情况。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [158] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI辅助AI研发可能引发的"智能爆炸"的数学动力学，指出奇异性增长（垂直渐近线）比经济学启发的模型所预测的更难实现，并识别出一类被忽视的"超指数但无垂直渐近线"的增长速率，强调"代际时间"（完成一轮反馈循环所需时间）是关键但常被忽略的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI被越来越多地用于辅助AI研发，这一自我改进的反馈回路在特定条件下可能导致智能爆炸（能力指数级加速提升）。作者希望通过数学建模深入理解驱动这一动力学的核心因素，特别是检验奇异性增长（垂直渐近线）是否如经济学启发的模型所暗示的那样容易实现。

Method: 采用数学建模方法，分析AI自我改进反馈回路的动力学。作者系统地探索了最"爆炸性"的增长可能性，对比了经济学启发的现有模型，并引入"代际时间"作为关键参数进行分析，考察其如何决定智能爆炸的行为特征。

Result: 研究结果表明：（1）奇异性增长（趋向垂直渐近线）比近期经济学启发模型所预期的更难实现；（2）存在一类重要的、被忽视的增长速率——快于指数增长但不会产生垂直渐近线；（3）代际时间是决定智能爆炸行为的关键参数，除非代际时间迅速趋近于零，否则无法实现奇异性增长。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的数学动力学比现有建模更为微妙。奇异性增长需要极为苛刻的条件（代际时间迅速趋零），而在超指数增长与垂直渐近线之间存在一个未被充分认识的增长速率区间的光谱。代际时间应作为未来AI自我改进动力学建模中不可忽视的核心参数。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [159] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE-Bench基准，包含144个跨六个物理域的源到目标环境适应任务，用于评估自进化agent在环境条件变化下的适应与恢复能力。基准远未饱和，且发现基于模拟器反射的方法优于未经验证的自我修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化agent评估通常在固定执行条件下进行优化，无法测试当执行条件变化后agent的恢复能力。作者认为这是关键评测缺口，因为真实世界中环境条件可能随时改变，agent需要具备适应能力。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench基准，包含144个源到目标适应对，覆盖六个物理域。每对将源环境链接到具有相同目标和接口的突变目标环境，源环境下成功的代码设计在目标环境下失败，agent需在有限尝试预算内利用诊断沙盒反馈迭代适应。比较了四类范式的十种自进化方法。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B在完整基准上仅成功35.9%的配对，GPT-5.5在全预算下解决Statics子集66.7%的任务。结果显示基于模拟器反射的方法比未经验证的自我修正更可靠，记忆机制会将agent锚定在早期设计上，而宽泛的树搜索探索不收敛。即使揭示精确物理变化也不能提升性能上限。

Conclusion: 机制重设计而非参数推断是自进化agent适应的核心瓶颈。基准和数据代码已公开，为未来自进化agent在物理环境变化下的适应能力研究提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [160] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，用于自动生成有据可依的多模态技术报告，集成了图片、表格和文本及支持性引用，并通过自动修订机制确保内容可溯源。人工评估显示其图表信息量在87%案例中优于基线，报告实用性在63%-100%案例中被评为更高；自动评估中引用召回率提升2.3倍、引用精确率提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的创新时代，知识增长速度极快，难以跟上；虽然生成模型越来越多地用于内容合成，但往往缺乏信息依据（grounding），无法确保生成内容的可靠性和可追溯性。

Method: 提出Wyvern多智能体框架，支持生成多模态输出，将图像、表格、文本与支持性引用集成到统一报告中；重点实现内容依据（grounding），包含声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段。

Result: 人工评估中，Wyvern生成的图表信息量在87%案例中被认为优于近期基线；报告实用性在63%至100%的实例中被评为高于三种替代方法。自动评估显示，相对于基线，Wyvern的引用召回率提升最多2.3倍，引用精确率提升最多1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern框架在生成有据可依的多模态技术报告方面表现出色，在图表信息量、报告实用性及引用质量（召回率和精确率）上均显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [161] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SheetCompass框架，通过显式建模工作表内外的结构关系并维护任务相关记忆，解决LLM在复杂电子表格推理中因扁平化序列化而丢失空间语义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格广泛应用但自动化推理对LLM具有挑战性。现有方法将多维结构扁平化为序列字符串，丢失了表内边界和跨表语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家在审查电子表格时天然依赖的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass是一个图引导且记忆驱动的智能体框架，显式建模工作簿内外的结构关系（跨表关联、列依赖、空间布局），并在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够更有效地对复杂工作簿进行推理。

Result: 论文摘要未提供具体实验结果数据，但框架设计旨在提升LLM在复杂电子表格推理和自动化任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 通过图结构建模和记忆驱动机制，SheetCompass能够弥补现有方法在电子表格多维结构和空间语义上的不足，从而增强LLM对复杂工作簿的推理与自动化能力。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [162] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种移位感知的双编码器迁移学习框架，用于解决目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性不同时的PM2.5短期预测问题。框架结合源域知识（美国10个监测站预训练）与目标域（台湾77个站点）特定表示学习，在冻结源编码器情况下优于基线模型，且消融实验表明目标域分支至关重要，允许源编码器自适应学习可获得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时，仅用本地数据训练的模型可能无法捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习又可能导致负迁移（negative transfer）。因此需要一种既能利用源域知识又能保留目标域特定信息的迁移框架。

Method: 提出移位感知双编码器（shift-aware dual-encoder）迁移框架：源编码器在美国10个监测站的逐时观测数据上预训练，然后结合目标域（台湾77个站点，两年逐时观测）特定的表示学习分支进行适应。采用按时间顺序的train-validation-test协议，并进行了四组主基线的对比、消融实验（移除台湾特定分支）以及SHAP分析。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四种基线中表现最佳：MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725，相比TL-v1和TL-v2分别降低约7.1%和4.1%的MSE。允许源编码器自适应学习时取得最优总体结果：MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739。消融实验显示移除台湾特定分支导致性能最大幅度下降。SHAP分析表明预测主要由近期PM2.5观测及与污染物传输和扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特定信息且允许迁移表示在目标域监督下自适应调整时最为有效。双编码器框架成功避免了负迁移，同时实现了优于纯局部训练和直接迁移学习的性能。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [163] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Test-time World-model Inference (Twin)系统，通过让前沿编码智能体编写可执行的"世界模型"来完成持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）。系统仅通过模拟和交互自动推断游戏规则与目标，利用重放验证和反例修复机制，在183个关卡中清关179个（97.8%），其中158个关卡（88.3%）比人更高效。该系统将基础模型得分从7.8%提升至93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法解决持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI游戏）需要为每个任务手工设计定制模型，人力成本高且无法泛化。每个游戏隐藏其规则和目标，需要自动化地从交互中推断这些信息，以实现真正的通用持续学习。

Method: Twin系统包含三个关键组件：(1) 一个前沿编码智能体负责编写可执行的世界模型；(2) 孪生（twin）世界模型中的重放验证机制，强制在动作执行前程序必须复现所有已观察到的游戏过渡；(3) 反例驱动的修复循环，模型预测与实际动作结果不匹配时生成反例用于修复世界模型。系统对网格游戏的归纳先验足够强，可从模拟和交互中恢复真实转移规则和目标。

Result: Twin系统清关183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），其中158/179（88.3%）比人类首次游戏更高效。系统在156个关卡（87.2%）在获得任何奖励前就推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现目标。基准评分（0-100，衡量完成度和动作效率）显示：基础模型直接玩仅7.8%，现成harness提升到61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升至93.3%，清关25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预期简单，而真正困难的问题在于推断正确的目标。世界模型驱动的测试时推理是提升大模型在持续学习任务上表现的有效范式。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [164] [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)
*Zhelun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"解释来源"与"整合结论"两个操作分离的框架，核心是四字段证据元组（假设、可靠性桶、理由、溯源）。作者发现并命名了"计数尺度漂移"这一失败模式：对未归一化权重的求和做阈值化等价于后验阈值化，但操作点会随来源数量滑动。解决方案是池化校准的对数似然比（算术修复而非架构修复），该方案适用于语言模型之外更广泛的评分求和类系统。作者在纵向语料上两次实例化该原则（结果确定后与结果确定前），后者通过小型序列编码器加树集成达到0.921 AUPRC（对比手工基线0.805），并给出五个可证伪预测与三个负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将多来源证据直接拼接进一个提示词让语言模型得出结论，这混淆了两种需求不同的操作：解释来源需要容量和上下文，而整合解释需要固定算术、跨实例可比性和返回空结果的选项。作者希望分离这两个操作并明确其接口，以揭示和修复此类系统中的系统性失败模式。

Method: 提出四字段证据元组（假设、可靠性桶、理由、溯源）作为两个操作之间的固定接口；通过数学分析揭示计数尺度漂移问题；提出池化校准对数似然比的修复方案；在纵向语料上两次实例化该原则（结果确定后与结果确定前），后者采用小型序列编码器加携带删失生存损失的树集成。

Result: 计数尺度漂移随阅读者可靠性增长；当来源可靠性不同时，投票规则与后验排序不一致且无阈值可调和；池化校准对数似然比解决两个问题。结果确定前的实例化达到0.921 AUPRC，对比手工基线0.805。同一划分在两种情况下都有帮助，但粒度不同（前者在阅读层面，后者在学习容量层面）。

Conclusion: 分离解释与整合是可行的，且通过固定证据元组即可同时确定两个半部分；修复是算术性的而非架构性的，适用于语言模型之外的广泛评分求和类系统。作者分离了跨域可迁移的成分与必须按域重新估计的成分，并给出五个可证伪预测、三个负面结果及未消除混淆的比较。

Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differently, and no threshold reconciles them. Pooling calibrated log-likelihood ratios addresses both problems. The fix is arithmetic rather than architectural, and applies to a class of rules beyond language models: score-summing triage engines, diagnostic panels scored by counting positives, and additive multi-signal detectors. We then instantiate the principle twice on one longitudinal corpus, once after outcomes resolve and once before. The same partition helps in both, at different granularities: over reading in the first, over learning capacity in the second. There, a small sequence encoder on an easy auxiliary objective plus a tree ensemble carrying the censored survival loss reaches 0.921 AUPRC against 0.805 for a hand-crafted baseline. We separate what transfers from what must be re-estimated per domain, and state five predictions that would falsify the framework, three negative results, and which comparisons remain confounded.

</details>


### [165] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两项研究（N=809）和三种部署场景（AI肾脏分配、AI模拟缺勤员工、生成式AI再现逝者），系统考察了道德AI偏好诱导管线的三个关键选择——特征范围界定、选民抽样和问题框架——如何系统性影响聚合后的道德偏好结果，表明投票式AI对齐无法仅靠聚合实现公平透明的AI，需对各阶段进行审计和披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地做出道德负载决策，道德偏好诱导（通过民意投票训练AI政策）成为常见回应方式。然而，开发者在投票前做出的三个关键选择（特征范围界定、选民抽样、问题框架）往往被视为技术细节而非规范性选择，且不透明、未记录。作者旨在揭示这些选择如何实质性影响诱导出的道德偏好。

Method: 研究采用两阶段实验设计（N=809），覆盖三个部署情境（AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟缺勤员工、生成式AI再现逝者）。针对道德偏好诱导的三个阶段分别检验：（1）特征范围——考察各情境中道德相关特征的跨情境迁移性；（2）选民抽样——按政治意识形态细分偏好差异；（3）问题框架——操纵问题措辞观察意识形态差距的收窄或扩大及其对道德基础关联性的影响。

Result: （1）道德相关特征在不同情境间存在差异，特征模式不能跨部署领域迁移；（2）约三分之一的特征显示出按政治意识形态区分的偏好差异，且部分差异方向相反，选民池的意识形态构成会改变聚合偏好画像；（3）问题措辞可将意识形态差异收窄或扩大多达一个完整量表点，框架条件也改变了道德基础与参与者判断的关联方式。

Conclusion: 投票式对齐无法仅靠聚合本身达成公平或透明的AI。道德偏好诱导管线中的每个阶段（特征范围界定、选民抽样、问题框架）都承载了规范性判断，应至少对其进行审计并向外界披露。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Refined outer-approximation algorithms for monotonic optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13694)
*Ahmed Rashwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对多块体外逼近算法（POA）的三项算法改进——广义锚点选择（最优平衡单调性切割）、无需连续性假设的松弛最优性条件、以及向量化多节点并发处理，并开发了高效的基于树的实现来加速核心子程序并紧凑存储外逼近。实验表明这些改进相比标准POA带来了数量级的加速，能解决现有变体无法处理的问题。作者还发布了开源Python包polyblocks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调优化是一类广泛的非凸问题，通常通过polyblock外逼近算法（POA）求解。然而POA的可扩展性差：描述逼近所需的顶点数量可能指数级增长，导致内存占用大且子程序越来越昂贵，这限制了其在实际问题中的应用。

Method: 提出三项算法改进：(1) 广义锚点选择，产生最优平衡单调性切割；(2) 松弛的最优性条件，在不需连续性假设下保证有限终止；(3) 向量化变体，并发处理多个节点。此外设计了基于树的实现来加速POA核心子程序并紧凑存储外逼近。

Result: 数值实验表明，这些改进相比标准POA实现了数量级的加速，并能解决现有变体无法求解的问题。

Conclusion: 三项算法改进和基于树的实现显著提升了POA的可扩展性和效率，并发布了开源的Python包polyblocks（含算法实现及开发新POA变体的框架），促进该领域的进一步研究与实践应用。

Abstract: Monotonic optimisation is a broad class of non-convex problems formulated in terms of monotone functions. Such problems are commonly solved via the polyblock outer-approximation algorithm (POA), a branch-and-bound method that iteratively refines a rectangular outer-approximation of the feasible set. POA scales poorly, however: the number of vertices needed to describe the approximation can grow exponentially, leading to large memory requirements and increasingly expensive subroutines. To address these limitations, we propose three algorithmic improvements: a generalised anchor selection that yields an optimal balanced monotonicity cut, a relaxed optimality condition that guarantees finite termination without continuity assumptions, and a vectorised variant that processes multiple nodes concurrently. We further develop an efficient tree-based implementation, which accelerates POA's core subroutines while storing the outer-approximation compactly. Numerical experiments show that these improvements yield order-of-magnitude speed-ups over standard POA and solve problems on which existing variants fail. Finally, we introduce polyblocks, an open-source Python package implementing the proposed algorithms alongside a framework for developing new POA variants, available at https://github.com/RashwanA/polyblocks.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-period Value-at-Risk Constrained Portfolio Optimization via DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13748)
*Thi Thu Van Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多期投资组合优化问题，该问题包含有限场景VaR约束、交易成本和多样化正则化。作者利用有限场景VaR-CVaR恒等式，在凸投资组合集上推导出带惩罚的差凸（DC）规划形式，并提出投影惯性Boosted差凸函数算法（iBDCA）来求解该非光滑非凸问题。算法结合了惯性外推、目标保护机制和增强线搜索，证明方法良定义、生成单调递减的目标序列、仅有临界聚点，并在局部无平局条件下建立整序列收敛性和局部R-线性收敛率。数值实验和样本外回测验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多期投资组合优化在实际中需同时考虑VaR风险约束、交易成本以及组合多样化，这些问题导致的目标函数是非光滑且非凸的，传统的凸优化方法无法直接求解，需要设计专门的算法来处理这种复杂结构。

Method: 利用有限场景VaR-CVaR恒等式将原问题转化为带惩罚的DC规划；提出投影惯性Boosted差凸函数算法（iBDCA），将惯性外推、目标保护（safeguard）和增强线搜索三种技术融入经典BDCA框架中，以加速收敛并保证迭代稳定性。

Result: 理论方面证明了算法良定义性、目标序列单调递减性以及聚点均为临界点；在局部无平局条件下建立了整序列收敛性和局部R-线性收敛率。数值实验中，iBDCA在与DCA和标准BDCA的对比中表现更优；样本外回测中与等权重和买入持有基准比较，展示了实际收益、风险、交易成本与VaR控制之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个理论上可证收敛且数值上有效的算法iBDCA，成功解决了带VaR约束的多期投资组合优化这一非光滑非凸问题，同时在理论与实证层面均验证了方法的优越性。

Abstract: We study a multi-period portfolio optimization problem with finite-scenario Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraints, transaction costs, and diversification regularization. Using a finite-scenario VaR--CVaR identity, we derive a penalized difference-of-convex (DC) formulation over the underlying convex portfolio set. To solve the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex problem, we propose a projected inertial Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm (iBDCA) that combines inertial extrapolation, an objective safeguard, and a boosted line search. We prove that the method is well defined, generates a monotonically decreasing objective sequence, and has only critical accumulation points for the penalized DC problem. Under a local no-ties condition at an accumulation point, we further establish whole sequence convergence with a local \(R\)-linear rate. Numerical experiments compare iBDCA with DCA and standard BDCA on matched problem instances. Out-of-sample backtests additionally include equal-weight and buy-and-hold benchmarks to illustrate the trade-offs among realized return, risk, transaction costs, and empirical VaR control.

</details>


### [168] [Best Response Dynamics for Zero-Sum Dynamic Games with Partial-Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13755)
*Yuxiang Guan,Iman Shames,Tyler H. Summers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分且非对称信息下的零和随机线性二次动态博弈（LQDGs），提出基于最优反应动态的求解方法，推导了纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略下各玩家的显式最优反应表达式，并通过数值实验发现博弈价值仅需少数几次迭代即可收敛，表明高阶信念状态的收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息不对称给LQDGs带来了信念表征和心智理论（theory of mind）的根本性挑战——玩家必须推断其他玩家的信念状态和估计值来制定策略。现有工作表明动态规划类分解方法难以应用于此类问题，因此需要替代性求解框架。

Method: 提出基于最优反应动态（best response dynamics）的替代方法。在纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略类（每个控制器的内部状态维度为系统状态维度的整数倍）中推导各玩家的显式最优反应表达式；玩家迭代更新最优反应时形成逐步高阶的信念状态，并辅以数值实验分析各种因素对博弈价值的影响。

Result: 数值结果表明博弈价值在仅数次迭代后即收敛，说明高阶信念状态带来的收益可忽略不计。实验还分析了非对称信念、信念阶数、相对可控性与可观测性以及直接前馈对线性二次追逃博弈价值的影响。

Conclusion: 最优反应动态为部分非对称信息下的LQDGs提供了可行且高效的求解途径；现实中较低阶信念状态可能已足够接近均衡，无需追求完全无限维的心智推理。

Abstract: This work studies a class of zero-sum stochastic linear quadratic dynamic games (LQDGs) under partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges related to \textit{belief representation} and \textit{theory of mind}, where players must impute belief states and estimates of other players to inform their strategies. Existing work highlights the difficulty of applying dynamic programming-like decomposition approach to these problems. An alternative approach based on \textit{best response dynamics} is proposed, which provides insights into belief representation and theory of mind challenges. Explicit expressions for each player's best response within the class of pure linear dynamic output feedback control strategies are derived, where the internal state dimension of each control is an integer multiple of the system state dimension. As players iteratively update their best responses, they form increasingly higher-order belief states, leading to infinite-dimensional internal states. However, numerical results reveal that the game's value converges after only a few iterations, suggesting that higher-order belief states provide vanishing benefit. This work further conducts numerical experiments to analyze the impact of asymmetric beliefs, belief orders, relative controllability and observability, and direct feed-through on a linear quadratic pursuit-evasion game's value.

</details>


### [169] [Recursive Filtering and Stochastic Control under Finite Partition-Based Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13825)
*Saul Díaz-Infante Velasco,Yofre H. Garcia,Jesús Adolfo Minjárez-Sosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种针对部分可观测随机系统的滤波与最优控制框架，其中每个观测识别隐藏状态空间有限可测划分中的一个类别，覆盖了阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等机制，并通过类受限条件测度构造信息状态，将原问题转化为完全可观测的马尔可夫决策过程，同时提出了类相关的有限维近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测随机系统的控制问题广泛存在，但现有框架对"区域信息"（即观测仅识别状态属于某个类别而非给出具体值）这一结构缺乏统一的处理方式。此类结构涵盖工程中常见的阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等多种机制，且可观测类可能包含原子、连续或混合分量，传统方法难以统一建模和求解。

Method: 论文首先在测度层面构建框架：对每个可观测类定义类受限的非归一化条件测度，通过其预测概率进行归一化得到后验分布。基于该递推关系，引入由观测类及其上的条件测度组成的信息状态，将原问题转化为完全可观测的马尔可夫决策过程；随后建立折扣代价准则下的贝尔曼方程，并给出平稳最优策略存在的条件。针对信息空间的无穷维特性，提出类相关的有限维近似（保留连续分量和原子质量），并推导出近似滤波误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界。

Result: 论文建立了统一的滤波-控制框架，成功将区域信息机制下的部分可观测控制问题转化为完全可观测MDP，证明了平稳最优策略的存在性条件，并提出了能同时保留连续分量与原子质量的类相关有限维近似方法，以及连接滤波误差与值函数误差的抽象误差界。

Conclusion: 该框架为处理具有区域观测结构的部分可观测随机系统提供了统一的理论基础和数值求解途径，并通过直方图近似的参考模型验证了构造的可行性。

Abstract: We develop a filtering and optimal-control framework for partially observable stochastic systems in which each observation identifies a class of a finite measurable partition of the hidden state space. This structure covers regional-information mechanisms associated with threshold, quantized, censored, event-triggered, and intermittent observations, and allows observable classes with atomic, continuous, or mixed components. The formulation is constructed first at the level of measures: for each observable class, we define a class-restricted unnormalized conditional measure, and the posterior distribution is obtained by normalizing with its predictive probability. Based on this recursion, we introduce an information state consisting of the observed class and the conditional measure supported on it, thereby transforming the original problem into a fully observable Markov decision process. We formulate the discounted-cost criterion, derive the Bellman equation, and establish conditions for the existence of stationary optimal policies. To address the infinite-dimensional nature of the information space, we propose class-dependent finite-dimensional approximations capable of preserving both continuous components and atomic masses. We also derive an abstract bound linking the error of the approximate filter to the error of the value function. A reference model illustrates the construction through histogram-based approximations

</details>


### [170] [Nondegeneracy Conditions for Control Problems with Nonregular Mixed Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13834)
*Jorge Becerril,Karla Cortez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带单一非正则混合不等式约束的最优控制问题中乘子的非退化性与正规性条件，其中乘子以纯有限可加集函数（charges）形式出现。作者证明了在单点非正则情形下退化是本质且不可避免的，并提出了四个可验证的条件来逐步保证非退化乘子、纯电荷消失、正规性以及电荷质量的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正则约束下最优控制问题的必要条件是病态的：退化解本质上不可避免，且正则逼近的规范化乘子在弱*拓扑下不存在L^∞中的极限，只能收敛到纯电荷。这一现象使得经典的必要条件失去区分可行过程的能力，因此需要系统地分析退化机制并寻找保证非退化与正规性的可验证条件。

Method: 首先针对单一时刻非正则的模型问题，严格证明退化是内在且不可避免的（每个可行过程都通过纯电荷退化地满足必要条件），并证明正则逼近的规范化乘子在Banach极限意义下仅能产生纯电荷。基于此分析，提出四个递进的可验证条件，分别保证非退化乘子的存在、纯电荷的消失、正规性以及电荷质量的显式上界。此外，在终端非正则时刻建立平衡恒等式，将电荷质量与代价乘子联系起来，从而判定完全异常或正规。

Result: 建立了非正则最优控制问题中退化性的不可避免性定理；提出了四个逐级增强的条件以克服退化并保证正规性；在终端非正则情形下导出了电荷质量与代价乘子之间的平衡恒等式；给出实例验证了各条件的可实现性并讨论了其局限性。

Conclusion: 非正则混合约束下退化解是本质现象，但通过精心设计的可验证条件可以在广泛的设定下恢复非退化性和正规性，从而为深度学习框架之外的最优控制理论上提供可操作的正规性判定准则。

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. We first prove for a model problem nonregular at a single instant that degeneration is intrinsic and unavoidable: every admissible process satisfies the necessary conditions degenerately via a pure charge, and normalized multipliers of natural regular approximations admit no weak$^*$ limit in $L^\infty([0,1];\mathbb{R})^*$, yielding only pure charges under every generalized limit, in the sense of Banach limits. Motivated by this analysis, we propose four verifiable conditions that progressively guarantee nondegenerate multipliers, the vanishing of the pure charge, normality, and explicit bounds on charge mass. Furthermore, we show that at a nonregular terminal instant, a balance identity ties the charge mass to the cost multiplier, deciding between complete abnormality and normality. We provide examples confirming the realizability and discussing the limits of each condition.

</details>


### [171] [First-Order Optimization as Minimum-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13915)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Pramod P. Khargonekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将一阶优化表述为最小时间控制问题：迭代点是状态，梯度组合是控制，目标集是梯度范数满足容差的点。值函数衡量单个实例的复杂度而非最坏情况复杂度。在强凸二次目标上，共轭梯度迭代由离散Pontryagin条件涌现，值即可控性指标；超越二次目标时，Hessian生成的可达跨度取代可控性矩阵，可达性决定临界点能否及多少步达到。曲率作为资源，可验证条件证明实例所需步数可以小于其在极小点处二次模型的可控性指标，且间隙可随维度无界增长。该值函数成为衡量任一阶方法的实例内在难度基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化复杂度分析采取最坏情况（worst-case）框架，无法反映具体实例的真实难度。作者希望建立一种逐实例（instance-wise）的复杂度度量，将优化与最优控制理论联系起来，从而为任意一阶方法提供针对具体问题的性能基准。

Method: 将一阶优化建模为最小时间控制问题，借助Pontryagin最小值原理推导最优更新律；在强凸二次情形下，离散Pontryagin条件自然涌现共轭梯度迭代，值函数被识别为可控性指标；对非二次目标，用Hessian张成的可达跨度替代可控性矩阵，以可达性理论判定临界点可达到性与所需步数，并给出基于曲率的可检验条件来界定实例复杂度。

Result: 在强凸二次问题上，最小时间控制框架精确重现了共轭梯度算法，值函数等价于可控性指标；在一般非二次问题上，可达跨度决定实例复杂度，且存在一个可检验条件，证明某些实例所需的步数严格小于其极小点处二次模型的可控性指标，该间隙可随维度无界增大。

Conclusion: 该工作为优化问题提供了全新的逐实例复杂度视角，建立了优化与最优控制的深层联系，使得任一阶方法可以在每个具体实例上被公平度量。曲率被揭示为决定实例内在难度的核心资源。

Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>


### [172] [Benders Decomposition with Partial Non-Anticipativity Relaxation for Multi-Stage Stochastic Clean Energy Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14105)
*Ahmet Emir Şener,Burak Kocuk,Tuğçe Yüksel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了校园级电-热综合能源系统的清洁能源转型规划问题，提出了一种多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，并开发了带部分非预期性松弛的Benders分解算法来高效求解大规模模型，通过土耳其中东技术大学校园案例验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园级综合电热系统的清洁能源转型规划面临战略层面和运行层面的双重不确定性（如技术成本与效率的随机演化、需求与可再生能源出力的短期波动），现有方法在求解高时间分辨率的大规模问题时计算负担过重，难以在合理预算内获得最优投资方案。

Method: 提出了多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，联合优化可再生能源发电、储能和热转换技术的投资与运行决策；基于箱式不确定集推导出鲁棒对等形式以应对短期运行不确定性；开发了带部分非预期性松弛的Benders分解算法，将投资变量及其非预期性约束保留在主问题中，运行变量分配到情景子问题并松弛其非预期性约束，最后通过小规模线性规划在终止时恢复非预期性；并加入有效不等式和两阶段切平面添加策略提升计算性能。

Result: 在土耳其中东技术大学校园案例中，所提方法在高时间分辨率下于合理计算预算内完成了求解，而扩展形式或经典Benders分解方法无法实现。通过滚动时域蒙特卡洛模拟和样本外分析表明，引入鲁棒性可显著提升转型计划的运行可靠性，同时总成本增加幅度适中。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法有效地平衡了计算效率和解的质量，证明了在综合能源系统转型规划中考虑运行不确定性带来的鲁棒性具有显著价值，为大规模随机优化问题提供了可行的求解途径。

Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

</details>


### [173] [Joint Inventory Placement, Assortment Personalization, and Order Fulfillment for Substitutable Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14275)
*Mikhail Fadin,Omar El Housni,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在线零售网络中联合优化库存配置、个性化商品推荐和订单履约的问题，提出具有理论保证的近似算法。在一般概率选择模型下实现渐近最优，在MNL模型下实现常数因子近似（0.080至0.199）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线零售商依赖分布式仓库网络快速履行订单，但库存配置、个性化推荐和订单履约这三个关键决策传统上被分开处理，缺乏联合优化的有效算法。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供首个可证明高效的联合优化算法。

Method: 在销售期开始时决定跨仓库的库存配置，随后不同类型的顾客按已知分布离散到达。每个顾客类型根据离散选择模型从推荐商品集合中最多选择一个商品，公司再从可行仓库中选择一个履约仓库，消耗库存并获得利润。作者为一般概率选择模型和MNL选择模型分别设计了近似算法，并进行了理论分析和实证评估。

Result: 在一般概率选择模型下，当仓库容量和产品供应规模扩展时，建立了渐近最优的近似算法。在MNL选择模型下，获得常数因子近似算法，近似因子在最一般情形下为0.080，在平稳性和无限产品供应（仅存在仓库容量约束）条件下可达0.199。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对联合库存配置、个性化商品推荐和订单履约问题提供可证明高效算法的研究，在不同模型假设下分别实现渐近最优和常数因子近似，具有强理论保证和良好实证表现。

Abstract: Modern online retailers leverage networks of distributed warehouses to rapidly fulfill customer orders. We study a problem of jointly deciding (i) how to allocate inventories across warehouses in the network subject to warehouse capacity and product supply constraints, (ii) how to dynamically select personalized product assortments based on customer preferences and location, as well as real-time stock levels, and (iii) which warehouse to use to fulfill the product chosen by the customer to maximize expected profit. In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first provably efficient algorithms for the joint inventory placement, assortment personalization, and order fulfillment problem.

</details>


### [174] [Diffusion-Based Policies for Dynamic Control of Stochastic Processing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14289)
*Baris Ata,Jin Guang,J. Michael Harrison,Nian Si*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用处理网络模型的最优控制方法，通过构建布朗控制问题（BCP）的近似，利用数值计算方法求解高维问题（可达m=50），并将数值解转化为实际可实施的排队网络控制策略，在重流量参数区间内近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多类排队网络模型中，作业类别与处理活动一一对应，限制了建模的灵活性。本文旨在处理更一般的网络模型，其中同一类作业可以有多种处理方式，且输入流可以部分或全部被拒绝，同时需要联合优化输入控制、作业路由和调度决策。

Method: 将排队网络的最优控制问题近似为一个布朗控制问题（BCP），其状态空间为m维非负象限，控制为从有界多面体中选取的漂移向量。利用近期发展的计算方法在数值上求解BCP（维度可达m=50），然后将数值解转化为原排队系统可实现的控制策略。该方法替代了以往基于低维"等效工作负载"公式化的方法。

Result: 数值示例表明该方法在重流量参数区间内近似最优，且比之前的"等效工作负载"方法在可解释性和实现性方面具有优势，后者虽然计算高效但难以在原网络中解释。

Conclusion: 该BCP方法提供了一种既计算可行（支持高维）又易于在原始排队网络中解释和实现的最优控制策略求解途径。

Abstract: We consider a processing network model with $m$ job classes or buffers, exogenous input flows into some classes, $n$ processing activities, and $p$ servers. Each activity is either a specified server processing jobs of a specified class, or a fictional input server delivering jobs of a specified class; jobs change class in Markovian fashion after completing service. A standard multiclass queueing network, with its one-to-one correspondence between job classes and activities, is a special case, but our general model allows two or more ways to process a given class, and some or all input flows may be turned away at the system manager's discretion.
  Costs are linear: a holding cost per time unit for each class $i$ job in the system, and a rejection penalty for each class $i$ arrival denied access $(i=1,\ldots,m)$. The system manager makes input control, job routing, and order-of-service decisions to minimize expected discounted costs over an infinite horizon.
  We formulate an approximating Brownian control problem (BCP) whose state space is the $m$-dimensional nonnegative orthant; control is a drift vector chosen from a bounded polyhedral set, based on dynamic state observations. Using recently developed computational methods, the BCP can be solved numerically in dimensions up to at least $m=50$, and we explain how the numerical solution is translated into an implementable control policy for the queueing system of original interest.
  Previous work on heavy traffic diffusion approximations suggests that this policy is nearly optimal in the heavy traffic parameter regime, and numerical examples support that conjecture. We also discuss its advantage over an alternative approach, featured in our previous work, where the BCP is replaced by a lower-dimensional "equivalent workload formulation" that is computationally efficient but difficult to interpret in the network of original interest.

</details>


### [175] [Optimal Control with $L^\infty$ and Integral Cost Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14316)
*Madhu Dhiman,Veeraruna Kavitha,Nandyala Hemachandra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究同时包含累积成本（积分型）和峰值成本（上确界/L∞型）的组合控制问题，通过松弛控制框架建立最优存在性，并引入平滑逼近方法构造ε-最优纯控制解，最后以排队问题验证该方法可实现峰值拥塞与累积性能之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典控制问题通常分别使用积分成本刻画系统累积性能，或使用上确界/L∞成本刻画最坏情况下的峰值行为。然而在许多实际场景中，累积性能与峰值性能同时重要，自然而然地需要考虑将两者结合的目标函数。尽管每种准则都已被充分研究，但它们的组合尚未被深入探索，这正是本文致力于解决的空白。

Method: 首先在松弛框架下考虑控制问题，将控制视为概率分布，利用控制空间的紧性与凸性建立最优松弛控制的存在性，进而证明对任意ε>0存在ε-最优的纯（经典）控制。其次，由于上确界项引入的非光滑性导致动态规划原理是否成立不明朗，本文引入一族光滑逼近，使其转化为具有良定义最优（纯）解的标准控制问题；利用最大值定理（Maximum Theorem）证明光滑问题的纯控制解构成原问题的ε-最优解，且ε随光滑参数趋于零而趋于零。

Result: 建立了组合成本控制问题的最优存在性定理（松弛控制与ε-最优纯控制）；证明了光滑逼近方法的收敛性，即光滑问题的解在原问题意义下达到ε-最优；通过排队问题的数值或解析说明，展示了该方法能够实现峰值拥塞水平与累积性能之间所需的最优权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为同时含积分成本与上确界成本的组合控制问题提供了系统的存在性理论，并通过光滑逼近给出了可计算的求解途径。该方法不仅理论上完备，而且在实际问题（如排队系统）中验证了其可行性，为后续处理此类混合性能指标的控制问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Many control problems are classically formulated using integral costs that capture the cumulative performance of a system. Peak or worst-case behavior is captured via supremum or $L^\infty$-costs in another variety of control problems. When both cumulative and peak performance are important, it is natural to consider objective functions that combine the two costs. Although each criterion is well studied, their combination has not been explored extensively and we precisely work on such control problems.
  Towards establishing the existence, we first consider the relaxed framework, where control is considered using probability distributions. Using the well-known compactness and convexity properties of such control spaces, we establish the existence of an optimal relaxed control---we eventually establish the existence of an $ε$-optimal pure (or classical) control, for every $ε> 0.$
  Despite these existence results, computing optimal policies remains challenging due to the non-smoothness introduced by the supremum term, and it is not clear whether the dynamic programming principle holds for our combined problem. To address this, we introduce a family of smooth approximations that yield standard control problems with well-defined optimal (pure) solutions. Using Maximum Theorem, we establish that the solutions of the smooth problems among pure controls form $ε$-optimal for the original problem, with $ε$ tending to zero as the smoothing parameter converges to zero.
  Finally using the methods proposed in this paper, we study a queueing problem to illustrate (among others) that the required trade-off between peak congestion levels and cumulative performance can be achieved.

</details>


### [176] [Optimizing bounds for energy-constrained optimal cooling problems in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14334)
*Pedro Blöss Braga,Giovanni Fantuzzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维不可压缩流体最优控制问题，目标是在给定动能预算（由Péclet数衡量）下最大化冷却效率。作者利用拉格朗日对偶性建立对偶问题给出冷却效率上界，并通过半定规划层次离散化数值求解。论文还构造了对偶问题的可行点，证明了两个新的解析上界：一般域上E(Pe) ≲ Pe²，以及在圆盘/环形域中具有正方位角平均热源/汇分布时E(Pe) ≲ Pe²/ln²Pe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程和自然界中，利用流体流动增强冷却效率是一个重要问题。给定动能预算下如何最优地设计流动以最大化冷却性能（即最小化温度梯度的均方值），是流体力学中的经典最优控制问题。现有研究对圆盘区域给出了效率上界，但缺乏对一般二维域和更复杂热源/汇分布的推广，因此需要更通用的理论框架和数值方法。

Method: （1）通过拉格朗日对偶性将原始最优控制问题转化为良置的对偶问题，对偶问题的最优值给出冷却效率的上界；（2）利用离散化技术构造收敛的半定规划（SDP）层级来数值近似对偶问题；（3）在正方形和环形域上实现该数值方法，并利用问题对称性降低计算复杂度；（4）构造对偶问题的可行点，获得新的解析上界：一般域和任意热分布下E(Pe) ≲ Pe²；在圆盘和环形域中，当天热源/汇分布具有正方位角平均时，E(Pe) ≲ Pe²/ln²Pe。

Result: 论文在正方形和环形域上通过数值方法展示了冷却效率上界的计算结果，并证明了两个新的解析上界。这些结果推广并改进了已有的能量约束冷却流动在圆盘中的效率界，特别是ln⁻²Pe因子的引入显著改善了上界对Péclet数的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般二维域中的最优冷却问题提供了统一的数值-解析框架，通过拉格朗日对偶和半定规划给出可计算且可证明的冷却效率上界。新的解析界推广到任意域和任意热分布，并针对具有方位角平均正性的热分布在圆盘和环形域中获得了更优的Pe²/ln²Pe上界。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. Specifically, we prove that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe}) \lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}$ for arbitrary domains and heat distributions, and that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})\lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}/ \ln^2\mathrm{Pe}$ for cooling flows in disks and annuli with heat source/sink distributions with a positive azimuthal average. These results generalize and improve known efficiency bounds for energy-constrained cooling flows in a disk.

</details>


### [177] [A Shrinkage Path Heuristic for Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14336)
*Lingjun Meng,Ryan Cory-Wright,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种收缩路径启发式算法（shrinkage path heuristic），将Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题简化为连接样本平均近似（SAA）与经典鲁棒优化解的一维搜索，在显著降低计算成本的同时，能达到DRO大部分样本外性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein DRO是处理不确定决策问题的常用框架，但其标准的确定性重构通常包含非凸的内层子问题，求解困难。因此需要一种能够高效求解DRO问题的启发式方法。

Method: 提出收缩路径启发式算法：将DRO问题约简为在线段上的一维搜索，该线段连接SAA解（通常良性）和经典鲁棒优化解（更具挑战性但实际可解）。在典型设定下推导先验次优性界，对一般情形则通过对偶公式应用类似启发式获得后验界。

Result: 在多项日用品报童问题和预约调度问题上进行数值实验，结果表明收缩路径启发式分别能达到Wasserstein DRO相对于SAA的样本外性能改进的85-110%和45-70%，而计算成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 收缩路径启发式提供了一种计算高效的DRO求解途径，在保持大部分性能优势的同时大幅降低计算开销，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is a versatile and widely adopted framework for decision-making under uncertainty, yet its standard deterministic reformulations generally contain non-convex inner subproblems that are challenging to solve. To address this issue, we propose a shrinkage path heuristic that reduces the solution of a DRO problem to a one-dimensional search over the line segment connecting the (typically benign) sample average approximation (SAA) and the (more demanding but practically solvable) classical robust optimization solution. We derive a priori suboptimality bounds in stylized settings and, for the general case, a posteriori bounds obtained by applying a similar heuristic to a dual formulation. Numerical experiments on a multi-item newsvendor and an appointment scheduling problem show that the shrinkage path heuristic attains 85-110% (resp. 45-70%) of the out-of-sample performance improvements of Wasserstein DRO over SAA, at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [178] [AI-Assisted Discovery and Construction of a Counterexample to the Convergence of Three-Block ADMM with the Identity Matrix as its Third Constraint Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14396)
*Kenan Xu,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明三块ADMM即使在前两个块为强凸二次函数且第三块约束为恒等映射时仍可能不收敛，通过Codex辅助构造了一个周期为66的有界非收敛轨道的显式有理反例，并研究了乘子松弛对恢复收敛的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三块ADMM的直接扩展是否收敛一直未决，特别是第三约束块为恒等映射这一子类既没有一般收敛性证明也没有反例。解决这一开放问题对理解ADMM算法的适用范围至关重要。

Method: 利用Codex（GPT-5.6 Sol）构建显式有理反例候选，沿分段仿射化简路径验证，通过精确检查确认周期66的有界非收敛轨道；同时借助Codex工作流研究乘子松弛恢复收敛的条件，并对比Kimi Code（Kimi K3）独立发现的周期23证书。

Result: 给出了否定答案：直接三块ADMM在前两块为强凸二次函数时仍可能发散。具体地，构造的实例产生周期为66的有界非收敛轨道；乘子松弛方面，问题依赖的小步长可以恢复收敛，但不存在对全类统一有效的正相对步长。另外Kimi Code独立发现了等价的全恒等实例上的周期23吸引子。

Conclusion: 直接三块ADMM在不加额外假设时本质上不收敛，即使对强凸二次目标函数也不例外；乘子松弛只能在特定实例层面恢复收敛而无法普适。此外，不同的AI研究工具配置会影响所探索的数学对象和所寻求的证明路径，体现了工具选择对数学发现的影响。

Abstract: The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), as a landmark algorithm, has attracted tremendous research attention and extensive practical applications over the past two decades. It is well known that, although the two-block ADMM enjoys well-established theoretical convergence guarantees, its direct extension to the three-block case may fail to converge, as demonstrated by existing counterexamples [5]. However, to the best of our knowledge, the case in which the third constraint block is the identity remains unresolved: the existing literature gives neither a general convergence proof nor a counterexample for this subclass. In this paper, we give a negative answer: direct three-block ADMM may fail even when the first two blocks are strongly convex quadratics. Using Codex with GPT-5.6 Sol, we construct an explicit rational counterexample candidate and verify it along a piecewise-affine reduction path; exact checks show that direct three-block ADMM on this instance produces a bounded nonconvergent orbit of period 66. Within the same Codex workflow, we further guide a study of multiplier relaxation and clarify when convergence can be restored at the fixed-instance and class levels: a problem-dependent small dual step can restore convergence, whereas no positive relative step works uniformly over the whole class. Furthermore, we also test the recent Kimi Code with Kimi K3 model without the Codex candidate or project-specific route guidance; along a different path it produces an exact locally attracting period-23 certificate, convertible to an equivalent all-identity instance. The comparison suggests that different research-harness configurations can shape the mathematical objects explored and the certificates pursued.

</details>


### [179] [Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting for Differentiable Solver Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14470)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出残差控制的Douglas–Rachford分裂（RCDRS），一种用于锥线性规划的可微分求解器层。它通过将展开求解器视为反馈控制动态系统，自适应调整松弛和目标驱动参数，在保持投影分裂结构的同时提升解质量、可行性和下游决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微分求解器层通过固定深度展开将约束优化嵌入端到端学习系统，但必须权衡解质量、可行性和计算预算。固定深度的展开方法无法根据问题难度自适应调整计算资源，导致解质量或可行性受限。

Method: RCDRS将展开求解器建模为反馈控制的动态系统，引入因果控制器自适应调整松弛参数和目标驱动参数，同时保持Douglas–Rachford分裂的投影分裂结构。理论上证明每个固定可行块仍是平均松弛的DRS算子，并具有有限步不动点残差界；分析带安全措施的时变展开作为极限平均算子的可和扰动；从最终分裂状态恢复终端原始-对偶诊断。实验在混合锥基准和工程应用上验证性能。

Result: 在混合锥基准测试和工程应用上的实验表明，RCDRS相比现有方法在解质量、可行性和下游决策性能方面均有提升。

Conclusion: RCDRS通过反馈控制机制实现自适应可微分求解器层，在不牺牲投影分裂结构的前提下改善解质量和可行性，为端到端学习中的约束优化嵌入提供了一种新范式的理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Differentiable solver layers embed constrained optimization into end-to-end learning systems, but fixed-depth unrolling must trade off solution quality, feasibility, and computational budget. We propose Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting (RCDRS), a differentiable solver layer for conic linear programs. RCDRS treats an unrolled solver as a feedback-controlled dynamical system, where a causal controller adapts the relaxation and objective-drive parameters while preserving the projection-splitting structure of Douglas--Rachford splitting. Theoretically, we show that each fixed admissible block remains an averaged relaxed DRS operator and admits finite-step fixed-point residual bounds. We further analyze safeguarded time-varying rollouts as summable perturbations of a limiting averaged operator, and recover terminal primal-dual diagnostics from the final splitting state. Experiments on mixed-cone benchmarks and engineering applications show that RCDRS improves solution quality, feasibility and downstream decision performance. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RC-DRS-180C/.

</details>


### [180] [On quantitative sufficient second-order optimality conditions for elliptic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14525)
*Francisco Fuica,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了无Tikhonov正则化的半线性椭圆方程箱约束分布最优控制问题，提出了一种关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性的定量二阶最优性条件，并证明了该条件在状态与追踪数据扰动下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无Tikhonov正则化的分布式最优控制问题中，二阶变分是线性化状态的二次型，其曲率系数函数可能为零或变号，导致传统的二阶最优性充分条件难以验证或失效。因此需要建立一种定量的充分条件来保证二次型的强制性（coercivity），从而为最优控制的存在性与稳定性分析提供理论基础。

Method: 针对受半线性椭圆方程约束、带箱约束的分布式最优控制问题，作者给出了一个定量条件来刻画二阶变分（作为线性化状态上的二次型）在L²范数意义下的强制性。该条件不依赖Tikhonov正则化项，直接对曲率系数函数的退化情形进行处理，并据此推导二阶条件在状态与追踪数据扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 提出了一个定量条件，该条件蕴含二阶变分相对于线性化状态L²范数的强制性；进一步证明了在此强制性的基础上，二阶最优性条件在状态与追踪数据发生扰动时保持稳定。

Conclusion: 无Tikhonov正则化情形下，通过定量条件建立二阶变分的强制性，可获得二阶最优性条件对数据扰动的稳定性，为这类退化最优控制问题的理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, a quantitative condition for optimality for distributed optimal control problems with box-constraints that are subject to a semilinear elliptic equation is considered. An important property of the investigated optimal control problems is the absence of a Tikhonov regularization. It is well known that at a given control, the second variation is a quadratic form in the linearized state, and its curvature coefficient function may vanish or change sign. We present a quantitative condition that implies coercivity with respect to the $L^2$-norm of the linearized-states. As a consequence, the stability of the second-order condition under perturbations of the states and the tracking data is shown.

</details>
